7
Conclusion and Discussions

In this work, we studied cooperative TD learning in heterogeneous environments, where agents share a low-dimensional common structure but face diverse local dynamics. By combining subspace estimation with personalized value function updates, we established finite-time convergence guarantees with linear speedup under Markovian sampling and clarified the limitations imposed by standard assumptions.
We hope that our analysis framework and insights provide a foundation for future work on exploiting common structures in multi-agent reinforcement learning.

Impact Statement

This paper presents work whose goal is to advance the field
of Machine Learning. There are many potential societal
consequences of our work, none of which we feel must be
specifically highlighted here.

Appendices

Appendix A
Additional Related Work

MARL under homogeneous environments.

When the environments of all agents are homogeneous, it has been shown that the federated version of classic reinforcement learning algorithms can significantly alleviate the data collection burden on individual agents
(
39
;
19
)
. Specifically, the per-agent sample complexity decreases linearly in terms of the number of agents – an effect commonly referred to in existing literature as linear speedup.
Recent work has made significant progress in understanding federated RL under homogeneous environments.

(
39
)
studied federated Q-learning.
In addition to a linear speedup,
(
39
)
uncovered the blessing of heterogeneity in terms of state-action exploration – a completely different notion of heterogeneity from our focus.

(
28
)
studied the sample-communication complexity trade-off, and developed a new algorithm that achieves order-optimal sample and communication complexities.

Model/Policy personalization.

In FL, model personalization (often referred to as PFL) has garnered significant attention in recent years.
The success of personalization techniques depends on balancing the bias introduced by using global knowledge that may not generalize to individual clients, and the variance inherent in learning from limited local datasets. Popular PFL techniques include regularized local objectives
(
32
;
21
)
, local-global parameter interpolation
(
10
)
, meta-learning
(
13
;
15
)
, and representation learning
(
8
;
43
)
.
Extending these ideas of PFL to RL introduces an even greater level of difficulty that arises from the inherent sample correlation induced by sequential decision-making, and the non-stationarity of sample quality as the policy evolves.

In the RL literature, training personalized (or localized) policies has recently garnered considerable attention in the context of multi-agent Markov games or competitive MARL
(
14
;
46
;
27
;
48
;
9
)
, which depart fundamentally from our focus.
Specifically, in their setting, agents interact with one another within a shared environment, and the rewards received by individual agents are determined by the joint actions of all agents. Each agent aims to learn a localized policy that contributes to a joint policy across all agents, with the goal of maximizing its own welfare.
A commonly adopted objective among the participating agents is to learn some form of equilibrium, such as a Nash Equilibrium.
Departing from Markov games, we focus on cooperative MARL, where we try to avoid the curse of heterogeneity in the joint learning.

Appendix B
Notations and Preliminaries

Let
v_{0:t}^{k}
denote the Markovian trajectory up to time
t
generated under initial state distribution
d_{0}
, given policy
\pi
, and the local transition kernel
P^{k}
:

v_{0:t}^{k}=(s_{0}^{k},a_{0}^{k},s_{1}^{k},...,s_{t+1}^{k}),

and
v_{0:t}
denote the collection of Markovian trajectories generated by all agents:

\displaystyle v_{0:t}=\{v_{0:t}^{k}\}_{k=1}^{K}.

Similarly, we define
v_{0:(t,\ell)}^{k}
and
v_{0:(t,\ell)}
as the Markovian trajectory generated up to the
\ell
-th sample in time
t
.

Let
{\mathbb{E}}_{j}[X_{t}]
denote the conditional expectation of any random variable
X_{t}
given the collection of Markovian trajectories up to time
j
:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{j}[X_{t}]={\mathbb{E}}\left[X_{t}\mid v_{0:j}\right]={\mathbb{E}}\left[X_{t}\mid\{v_{0:j}^{k}\}_{k=1}^{K}\right].

Denote

\displaystyle\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})\phi(s_{t,0}^{k});\quad\bar{b}_{L}^{k}={\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})-J^{k})\phi(s^{(0)})\right].

(14)

In addition, let

\displaystyle\xi_{t,L}^{k}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})

(15)

\displaystyle+(\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})-J^{k})\phi(s^{(0)})\right]

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\phi(s^{(0)})\right],

where the expectation is taken with respect to

{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}
.
This can be viewed as the Markovian noise of the TD error of agent
k
at time
t
.
Moreover, let

\displaystyle\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}=\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top},

and

\displaystyle{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\big((r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\big)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}).

(16)

The following rewrite appears repeatedly in our analysis.

Proposition 1
.

For any
t
and agent
k
,
\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})
can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t}^{k})=\xi_{t,L}^{k}+A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}+Ly_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t}^{k}),

(17)

and

\displaystyle\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t}^{k})=\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}+Ly_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})+{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}.

(18)

The proof of Proposition
1
can be found in Appendix
I
.

Appendix C
Bound on QR Decomposition and Perturbation

Recall from Algorithm
1
the update of
\bar{\mathbf{B}}
:

\displaystyle\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}=\mathbf{B}_{t}+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}

For ease of exposition, let

\displaystyle{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}:=\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}.

(19)

The following couple of lemmas enable us to bound the “distortion” caused by the QR decomposition.
Specifically, Lemma
C.1
bounds the perturbation in terms of
\|\bf Q_{t}\|
, which is further bounded in Lemma
C.2
.
It is worth noting that, in the traditional PFL
(
8
)
, such distortion can be well controlled by the number of
i.i.d.
local samples that are freshly drawn in each iteration. However, their analysis does not apply to our setting due to the Markov sampling.
We fundamentally depart from the analysis in
(
8
)
in deriving upper bounds on those distortions via constructing a fixed-point iteration.

Lemma C.1

(Perturbation of QR)
.

For each
t\geq 1
, when
U_{\delta}U_{\omega}\zeta\leq 1/2
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|{{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}}\|_{2}\leq 2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2},

(20)

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}^{-1}\|\leq\frac{1}{1-\|{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}\|_{2}}\leq\frac{1}{1-2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}},

(21)

\displaystyle\|{{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{I}}}\|_{2}\leq 4\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}.

(22)

Proof.

Recall that
\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}={\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}
. We rewrite
{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}
as

\displaystyle{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}^{\top}\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}=\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}+\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{t}+\mathbf{Q}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}+\mathbf{Q}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{t}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{I}}+\mathbf{B}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{t}+\mathbf{Q}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}+\mathbf{Q}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{t}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{I}}+\mathbf{Q}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{t}.

The last equality holds because
{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}
lives in
\text{span}({\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp})
.

It can also be rewritten as

\displaystyle{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}^{\top}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=({\mathbf{I}}+{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}})^{\top}({\mathbf{I}}+{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}})

\displaystyle={\mathbf{I}}+({\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}})^{\top}+({\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}})+({\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}})^{\top}({\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}).

Thus, we get

({\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}})^{\top}+({\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}})=\mathbf{Q}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{t}-({\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}})^{\top}({\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}).

(23)

We construct a fixed-point iteration to “solve”
{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}
in terms of
\mathbf{Q}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{t}
under Eq. (
23
).

Toward this, we decompose the symmetric matrix
\mathbf{Q}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{t}
into

\mathbf{Q}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{t}=\mathbf{R}_{0}+\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\top},

where
\mathbf{R}_{0}
is upper triangular. It is easy to see that

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbf{R}_{0}}\right\|_{{F}}\leq\left\|{\mathbf{R}_{0}+\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\top}}\right\|_{{F}}\leq\|\mathbf{Q}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\zeta^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}.

Let
\Delta_{t+1}\triangleq{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{R}_{0}
.
Then Eq. (
23
) is equivalent to

\Delta_{t+1}^{\top}+\Delta_{t+1}=-(\Delta_{t+1}^{\top}\Delta_{t+1}+\Delta_{t+1}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{0}+\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\top}\Delta_{t+1}+\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{0}).

(24)

Next, we define a sequence of
\Delta_{t+1,i}
for
i\geq 0
.
Let
\Delta_{t+1,0}=0
for
i\geq 1
and let
\Delta_{t+1,i}
be an upper triangular matrix satisfying

\Delta_{t+1,i}^{\top}+\Delta_{t+1,i}=-(\Delta_{t+1,i-1}^{\top}\Delta_{t+1,i-1}+\Delta_{t+1,i-1}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{0}+\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\top}\Delta_{t+1,i-1}+\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{0}).

We first show the boundedness of the sequence.
Note that

\left\|{\Delta_{t+1,i}}\right\|_{{F}}\leq\left\|{\Delta_{t+1,i}^{\top}+\Delta_{t+1,i}}\right\|_{{F}}\leq\left\|{\Delta_{t+1,i-1}}\right\|_{{F}}^{2}+2\left\|{\mathbf{R}_{0}}\right\|_{{F}}\left\|{\Delta_{t+1,i-1}}\right\|_{{F}}+\left\|{\mathbf{R}_{0}}\right\|_{{F}}^{2},

(25)

where the second inequality applies the triangle inequality,
and the first inequality holds because the absolute value of all entries in
\Delta_{t+1,i}^{\top}+\Delta_{t+1,i}
is larger than that in
\Delta_{t+1,i}
.

Provided
\zeta^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}<1/4
, we have
\left\|{\mathbf{R}_{0}}\right\|_{{F}}<1/4
, and for any
\left\|{\Delta_{t+1,i-1}}\right\|_{{F}}\leq 1/4
, we have
\left\|{\Delta_{t+1,i}}\right\|_{{F}}\leq 1/4
by Eq. (
25
).
By induction, the entire sequence satisfies
\left\|{\Delta_{t+1,i}}\right\|_{{F}}\leq 1/4
for all
i\geq 0
.

Next, we show the convergence of the sequence
\Delta_{t+1,i}
.
By definition,

\displaystyle(\Delta_{t+1,i+1}^{\top}+\Delta_{t+1,i+1})-(\Delta_{t+1,i}^{\top}+\Delta_{t+1,i})

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-(\Delta_{t+1,i}^{\top}(\Delta_{t+1,i}-\Delta_{t+1,i-1})+(\Delta_{t+1,i}-\Delta_{t+1,i-1})^{\top}\Delta_{t+1,i-1}

\displaystyle\qquad+(\Delta_{t+1,i}-\Delta_{t+1,i-1})^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{0}+\mathbf{R}_{0}^{\top}(\Delta_{t+1,i}-\Delta_{t+1,i-1})).

By taking the norm on both sides, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|{\Delta_{t+1,i+1}-\Delta_{t+1,i}}\right\|_{{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|{(\Delta_{t+1,i+1}-\Delta_{t+1,i})^{\top}+(\Delta_{t+1,i+1}-\Delta_{t+1,i})}\right\|_{{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\left\|{\Delta_{t+1,i}-\Delta_{t+1,i-1}}\right\|_{{F}}+\frac{1}{4}\left\|{\Delta_{t+1,i}-\Delta_{t+1,i-1}}\right\|_{{F}}

\displaystyle\quad+2\left\|{\mathbf{R}_{0}}\right\|_{{F}}\left\|{\Delta_{t+1,i}-\Delta_{t+1,i-1}}\right\|_{{F}}.

Therefore, since
\left\|{\mathbf{R}_{0}}\right\|_{{F}}<\frac{1}{4}
, the difference
\left\|{\Delta_{t+1,i+1}-\Delta_{t+1,i}}\right\|_{{F}}
geometrically converges to zero, which implies that the sequence
\Delta_{t+1,i}
converges.

The limit of the sequence satisfies the fixed-point Eq.  (
24
).
Taking norm on both sides of Eq. (
24
) yields

\left\|{\Delta_{t+1}}\right\|_{{F}}\leq\left\|{\Delta_{t+1}^{\top}+\Delta_{t+1}}\right\|_{{F}}\leq\left\|{\Delta_{t+1}}\right\|_{{F}}^{2}+2\left\|{\mathbf{R}_{0}}\right\|_{{F}}\left\|{\Delta_{t+1}}\right\|_{{F}}+\left\|{\mathbf{R}_{0}}\right\|_{{F}}^{2}.

Since
\left\|{\Delta_{t+1}}\right\|_{{F}}\leq 1/4
, we obtain that

\left\|{\Delta_{t+1}}\right\|_{{F}}\leq\frac{1-2\left\|{\mathbf{R}_{0}}\right\|_{{F}}-\sqrt{1-4\left\|{\mathbf{R}_{0}}\right\|_{{F}}}}{2}\leq 4\left\|{\mathbf{R}_{0}}\right\|_{{F}}^{2}.

We conclude that

\left\|{{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}}\right\|_{{F}}=\left\|{\mathbf{R}_{0}+\Delta_{t+1}}\right\|_{{F}}\leq\left\|{\mathbf{R}_{0}}\right\|_{{F}}+4\left\|{\mathbf{R}_{0}}\right\|_{{F}}^{2}\leq 2\left\|{\mathbf{R}_{0}}\right\|_{{F}}\leq 2\left\|{{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}}\right\|_{{F}}^{2}.

Moreover,

\sigma_{\min}({\mathbf{R}}_{t+1})\geq 1-\|{{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}}\|_{2},~~~\text{by Weyl's inequality},

\sigma_{\max}({\mathbf{R}}_{t+1})\leq 1+\|{{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}}\|_{2},~~~\text{by triangle inequality},

and

\|{{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{I}}}\|_{2}=\|{{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}^{-1}({\mathbf{I}}-{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1})}\|_{2}\leq\|{{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}^{-1}}\|_{2}\|{{\mathbf{I}}-{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}}\|_{2}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|{{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}}\right\|_{{2}}\leq\left\|{{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}}\right\|_{{F}}\leq 2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2},

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}^{-1}\|\leq\frac{1}{1-\|{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}\|_{2}}\leq\frac{1}{1-\|{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}\|_{F}}\leq\frac{1}{1-2\left\|{\bf Q_{t}}\right\|_{{F}}^{2}},

\displaystyle\|{{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{I}}}\|_{2}\leq 4\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}.

For the last inequality, we used
2U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}\leq 1/2
so that
\|{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}^{-1}\|\leq 2
.

∎

Lemma C.2
.

For any
t\geq\tau
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{16U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+4\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+8U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\zeta^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}

\displaystyle+4\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}.

(26)

Proof.

For any
t\geq\tau
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}=\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}+{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}+Ly_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right)(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}~~~(\text{by Proposition \ref{prop: key intermediate}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\underbrace{\frac{4\zeta^{2}L^{2}}{K^{2}}\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}}_{I_{1}}+\underbrace{\frac{4\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}}_{I_{2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{4\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}}_{I_{3}}+\underbrace{\frac{4\zeta^{2}L^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}y_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}}_{I_{4}}.

Recall that
\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top},{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left((r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})
.
Then,

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}\|=\|\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\|\leq 2.

(27)

For
I_{1}
, we have,

\displaystyle I_{1}\leq\frac{4\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|^{2}\leq\frac{16U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}.

For
I_{2}
, the Markovian noise, we have under expectation that,

\displaystyle\frac{4\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\|^{2}+\frac{8\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{k^{\prime}=k+1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top},{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k^{\prime}}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k^{\prime}})^{\top}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+8\zeta^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}.~~~~~(\text{by Lemma \ref{lmm: U delta}, independence and the {ergodicity assumption}})

For
I_{3}
,

\displaystyle\frac{4\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\zeta^{2}\left(\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}\beta\right)^{2}~~~~~(\text{by Lemma \ref{lmm: crude parameter bound}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}.

Lastly, for
I_{4}
, we have

\displaystyle I_{4}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4\zeta^{2}}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|y_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|^{2}\leq\frac{4U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}.

In summary,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{16U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+4\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+8U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\zeta^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}

\displaystyle+4\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}.

∎

Appendix D
Bounds on Local Head Errors

D.1
Upper bound: Proof of Lemma
5.1

We first provide a formal statement of Lemma
5.1
, which involves a set of constants whose formal definitions are deferred to the lemma proof.

Lemma D.1

(Upper bound of local head errors)
.

Let
{\mathbf{P}}^{*}={\mathbf{B}}^{*}({\mathbf{B}}^{*})^{\top}
, and
{\mathbf{P}}_{t}={\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}
for each
t
. Choose
\tau=\lceil 2\log_{\rho}(\zeta)\rceil
,
U_{\delta}U_{\omega}\zeta\leq\frac{1}{2}
, where
U_{\delta}=2U_{r}+2U_{\omega}
,
\beta=c\zeta
for arbitrary
c>0
that can be tuned. There exist arbitrary positive constants
c_{3},c_{4},c_{5},c_{6}
such that

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{t+1}\leq\Bigg(1-2\lambda c\zeta+\Big(c_{3}^{2}\frac{c}{L}+c_{4}^{2}c+c_{5}^{2}\frac{1}{L}+c_{6}^{2}+4U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{1}{c_{5}^{2}L}\Big)\zeta+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,1}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,2}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{3}\Bigg)\bar{X}_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\frac{36U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{3}^{2}}\frac{c\zeta}{L}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c\zeta\right)M_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+\Bigg(\Big[\frac{1}{c_{4}^{2}}c+\frac{U_{\omega}^{4}}{c_{6}^{2}}\Big]\zeta+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,4}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{3}\Bigg)\bar{Y}_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,5}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,6}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,7}\zeta^{3}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,8}\zeta^{4}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,9}\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,10}\zeta^{5}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,11}\gamma^{2}\zeta^{3}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,12}\zeta^{7}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,13}\zeta^{6},

where

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,1}(\tau^{2})=\bigg(C_{31}c+(36+40\tau^{2})\frac{c^{2}}{L^{2}}+C_{33}\frac{c}{L}+\left(36U_{\omega}^{4}+\left(2.5+3U_{\omega}\right)C_{18}\right)\frac{1}{L^{2}}+\frac{96c^{2}}{L^{2}}+\frac{256U_{\omega}^{4}}{L^{2}}+96U_{\delta}c\frac{U_{\omega}^{3}}{c_{2}^{2}}\frac{1}{L^{2}}\bigg),

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,2}(\tau^{2})=6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,4}(\tau^{2}),

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}(\tau^{2})=\Bigg[C_{32}c+(9+10\tau^{2})c^{2}+C_{34}c+9U_{\omega}^{4}+\left(2.5+3U_{\omega}\right)C_{23}+24c^{2}+64U_{\omega}^{4}+24U_{\delta}c\frac{U_{\omega}^{3}}{c_{1}^{2}}\Bigg],

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,4}(\tau^{2})=6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,5}(\tau^{2}),{\mathcal{C}}_{X,5}(\tau^{2})=\left[C_{39}(\tau^{2})+24c^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})\right],{\mathcal{C}}_{X,6}(\tau^{2})=\left[C_{41}(\tau^{2})+64\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{4}\right],

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,7}=\left[C_{42}+C_{43}(\tau)+24U_{\omega}U_{\delta}cC_{25}(\tau^{2})\frac{1}{L}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2})\frac{1}{K}\right],

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,8}=\left[C_{40}(\tau^{4})+100U_{\omega}^{6}U_{\delta}^{4}+144U_{\omega}^{2}\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}c^{2}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,6}\right],{\mathcal{C}}_{X,9}=C_{45}(\tau^{4}),{\mathcal{C}}_{X,10}=6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,1},

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,11}=24U_{\omega}U_{\delta}cC_{28}(\tau^{4}),{\mathcal{C}}_{X,12}=6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,3},{\mathcal{C}}_{X,13}=\left[128U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}m^{2}+C_{44}(\tau^{2})\right],

and the missing definition of constants such as
C_{3}1
can be found in the analysis.

Proof.

Recall that
\omega_{t+1}^{k}=\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\omega_{t}^{k}+\frac{\beta}{L}\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))
,
{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}=\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}^{-1}
, and
\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}=\omega_{t}^{k}+\frac{\beta}{L}\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})
.
We have

\displaystyle\|x_{t+1}^{k}\|=

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-z^{k,*}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-z^{k,*}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-z^{k,*}+{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-z^{k,*}+{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}+{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|.

Taking the square on both sides, we get

\displaystyle\|x_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}=

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}+\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}

(28)

\displaystyle+2\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}\rangle.

We bound
\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}
as follows:

We have

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}+\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|+\|({\mathbf{I}}-{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1})\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\frac{\beta}{L}\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\|+\|({\mathbf{I}}-{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1})\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|

where the last inequality follows from Eq. (
20
) and we use
\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}\beta\leq U_{\omega}
for large
T
such that
\|\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|\leq 2U_{\omega}
.
Then,

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}\|\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\|^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{2}\|{\mathbf{Q}}\|_{F}^{4}.

For the first term, we further decompose it as:

\displaystyle\|\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\|^{2}\leq\|\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}+{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}+Ly_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\|^{2}~~~~(\text{by Proposition \ref{prop: key intermediate}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+3\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}+3\|Ly_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 12\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+3\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}+3L^{2}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}.

Taking conditional expectation over the Markovian trajectory up to
t-\tau
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 12{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+3{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}+3L^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 12{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+3U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})L+3L^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}.~~(\text{by Lemma \ref{lmm: local noise reduction} and tower property})

(29)

Finally, the second term in Eq. (
28
) can be bounded as

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 24\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+6\frac{\beta^{2}}{L}U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})+6\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{Q}}\|_{F}^{4}.

(30)

As for
2\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}\rangle
,

we first write
\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}
as

\displaystyle\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}=\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega^{*,k}={\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega^{k,*}+\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\rangle}_{I_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}\rangle}_{I_{2}}.

Both
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
can be well controlled by the projection error of
\widetilde{w}
and the perturbation error that arises from the QR decomposition of
\bar{{\mathbf{B}}}
.
We bound
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
separately.

On
I_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}+{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\left(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-\omega_{t+1}^{k}\right)+{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\left({\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}\right)\omega_{t+1}^{k}.

So,
I_{2}
can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\left(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-\omega_{t+1}^{k}\right)\rangle}_{I_{21}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\left({\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}\right)\omega_{t+1}^{k}\rangle}_{I_{22}}.

For
I_{21}
, by Cauchy Schwartz, we have

\displaystyle I_{21}\leq\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\left(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-\omega_{t+1}^{k}\right)\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\left(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-\omega_{t+1}^{k}\right)\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}+\|\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-\omega_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}.

For the last inequality, we use
\|{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\|\leq 2
for when
2U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}\leq 1/2
.
As for
\|\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-\omega_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}
, by Eq. (
D.1
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\frac{\beta}{L}\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 12\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+3\frac{\beta^{2}}{L}U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})+3\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}.

For
I_{22}
, we have

\displaystyle I_{22}\leq\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\left({\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}\right)\omega_{t+1}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\left({\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}\right)\omega_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\|{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{I}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}+2U_{\omega}^{2}\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{4}.~~~~\text{(by Eq.\,(\ref{eqn: R related bounds: R-I}))}

Combining
I_{21}
and
I_{22}
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{2}]\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+12\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+3\frac{\beta^{2}}{L}U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})+3\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}+2U_{\omega}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 36\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+9\frac{\beta^{2}}{L}U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})+9\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}+34U_{\omega}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{4}.~~~~~~\text{(by Eq.\,(\ref{eq: upper bound of qr-proj squared}))}

Bounding
I_{1}
is slightly more involved.
We have

\displaystyle I_{1}=\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\rangle

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\rangle}_{I_{11}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\rangle}_{I_{12}}

For
I_{11}
, let
{\mathcal{R}}={\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top},
where
{\mathbf{U}}\Sigma{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}={\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}
is the SVD. Note that
{\mathcal{R}}
is the optimal rotation matrix that aligns
{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}
and
{\mathbf{B}}^{*}
in the sense that
{\mathcal{R}}=\text{argmin}_{{\mathcal{R}}^{\top}{\mathcal{R}}={\mathbf{I}}}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\|_{F}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle I_{11}=\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}(\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}),{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}}^{-1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\rangle~~~(\text{by rotation})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}}^{-1}(\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}),({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}){\mathcal{R}}^{-1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}(\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}),{\mathbf{B}}^{*}({\mathcal{R}}^{-1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\omega^{k,*})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\mathcal{R}}^{-1}(\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}))^{\top}({\mathbf{I}}-({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}){\mathcal{R}}^{-1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}

\displaystyle+\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}}^{-1}(\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}),({\mathbf{B}}^{*}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})({\mathcal{R}}^{-1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\omega^{k,*})\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}}^{-1}(\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}),{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}}({\mathcal{R}}^{-1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\omega^{k,*})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\mathcal{R}}^{-1}(\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}))^{\top}({\mathbf{I}}-({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}){\mathcal{R}}^{-1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}

\displaystyle+\langle{\mathcal{R}}^{-1}(\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}),(({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}-{\mathbf{I}})({\mathcal{R}}^{-1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\omega^{k,*})\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},\omega_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathcal{R}}\omega^{k,*}\rangle.

We have the following facts:

•

Fact 1: Since the ball with radius
U_{\omega}
is convex, and
\omega^{k,*}
belongs to the ball, it holds that

\langle\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},\omega_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathcal{R}}\omega^{k,*}\rangle\leq 0

.

•

Fact 2:

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{I}}-({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\|\leq\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\|^{2}.

(31)

The proof of Fact 2 is postponed to Appendix
I.1
.

Therefore,

\displaystyle I_{11}\leq

\displaystyle\left\|{\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{{\mathbf{I}}-({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\omega_{t+1}^{k}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|{\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{{\mathbf{I}}-({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{{\mathcal{R}}^{-1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\omega^{k,*}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3U_{\omega}\left\|{\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}}\right\|_{2}\|m_{t+1}\|^{2}.

Similarly, for
I_{12}
, we have

\displaystyle I_{12}=\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}({\mathbf{I}}-{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1})\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 4U_{\omega}^{2}\|{\mathbf{I}}-{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\|

\displaystyle\leq 8U_{\omega}^{2}\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}.

Summing up
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
and multiplying by 2, we have

\displaystyle 2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 72\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+18\frac{\beta^{2}}{L}U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})+18\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}+68U_{\omega}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{4}

\displaystyle+6U_{\omega}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\|{\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}}\right\|_{2}\|m_{t+1}\|^{2}+16U_{\omega}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}.

(32)

We bound
\|\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}\|
as follows:

\displaystyle\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}

\displaystyle=\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-z^{k,*}

\displaystyle=\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\right)\left(\omega_{t}^{k}+\frac{\beta}{L}\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right)-z^{k,*}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z^{k,*}+\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}(\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad+\zeta\beta(\frac{1}{KL^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top})\left(\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right).

Squaring both sides, we have

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}=

\displaystyle\|\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-z^{k,*}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z^{k,*}\|^{2}+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z^{k,*},{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\rangle}_{I_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{2\zeta\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z^{k,*},\frac{1}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})\delta_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle}_{I_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{2\frac{\zeta\beta}{L^{2}}\left\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z^{k,*},(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top})\left(\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right)\right\rangle}_{I_{3}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{3\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}(\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))\|^{2}}_{I_{4}}+\underbrace{3\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\|\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\|^{2}}_{I_{5}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{3\frac{\beta^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{4}}\|(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top})\left(\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right)\|^{2}}_{I_{6}}.

(33)

We bound these terms separately.

Bounding
I_{1}
.
Roughly speaking, the main drift of the decay in
\|x_{t}^{k}\|
may arise from
2\beta\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t}^{k})\delta_{t}^{k}\rangle
(i.e.,
I_{1}
in Eq. (
D.1
)).
However, the environmental heterogeneity significantly complicates the characterization of this term. Specifically, by Proposition
1
,
I_{1}
can be written as:

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\delta_{t,L}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\xi_{t,L}^{k}\rangle+2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle+2\beta\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}y_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle}_{I_{11}}+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}\xi_{t,L}^{k}\rangle}_{I_{12}}+\underbrace{2{\beta}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}y_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\rangle}_{I_{13}},

(34)

recalling that
{\mathbf{P}}_{t}={\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}
. Inside
I_{1}
, the main negative drift arises from the term
\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle
. Intuitively, in traditional single-agent or homogeneous environment settings,
\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle\approx\langle x_{t}^{k},Ax_{t}^{k}\rangle
, which is mainly controlled by the spectrum of
A
, with the desired property directly assumed in Assumption
1
. However, in the presence of environmental heterogeneity, Assumption
1
does not directly guarantee a negative drift of the
x_{t}^{k}
due to the existence of
{\mathbf{P}}_{t}
and its intricate interplay with the heterogeneous
A_{L}^{k}
.
Specifically, (1)
{\mathbf{P}}_{t}
is not of full rank, (2) the local head error will be distorted in a different manner due to the product
{\mathbf{P}}_{t}A_{L}^{k}
, and (3)
{\mathbf{P}}_{t}
varies over time.
To address this, we further decompose
I_{11}
as

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}^{*})A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle+2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}^{*}A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle}_{I_{111}}+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}^{*})A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle}_{I_{112}}-\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{\perp}^{*}A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle}_{I_{113}}.

(35)

The first term of Eq. (
D.1
),
I_{111}
, is a contraction by Assumption
1
. Specifically,

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle\leq-2\lambda\beta\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}.

I_{112}
can be controlled via the principal angle distance between the subspace estimate
{\mathbf{B}}_{t}
and the underlying truth
{\mathbf{B}}^{*}
. Since

\displaystyle\|A_{L}^{k}\|\leq\mathbb{E}_{s^{0}\sim\mu^{k},a^{0}\sim\pi,...,s^{(L)}\sim P^{k}}\left\|{\phi(s^{(0)})(\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}}\right\|\leq 2,

(36)

it holds that

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}^{*})A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle\leq 8U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}^{*}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

The third term,
I_{113}
, can be bounded as

\displaystyle-2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{\perp}^{*}A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=-2\frac{\beta}{L}({\mathbf{P}}_{\perp}^{*}x_{t}^{k})^{\top}A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}=-2\frac{\beta}{L}({\mathbf{P}}_{\perp}^{*}\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right))^{\top}A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}

\displaystyle=-2\frac{\beta}{L}\left({\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right)\right)^{\top}A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}

\displaystyle=-2\frac{\beta}{L}({\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}

\displaystyle\leq 4\frac{\beta}{L}U_{\omega}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

(37)

wherein the local head error
x_{t}^{k}
and the principal angle distance
\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\|
are also coupled.
Then,

\displaystyle I_{11}\leq-2\lambda\beta\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+12U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

(38)

It is worth noting that when
\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\|=0
, i.e., when
{\mathbf{B}}_{t}
and
{\mathbf{B}}^{*}
span the same
r
-dimensional subspace, the second and the third terms in Eq. (
D.1
) become zero, and Eq. (
D.1
) reduces to the standard negative drift term. We postpone the characterization of the convergence of
\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\|
to Lemma
5.3
.

For
I_{13}
,we have

\displaystyle I_{13}\leq 2\beta\|x_{t}^{k}\||y_{t}^{k}|.

(39)

Recall
\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})\phi(s_{t,0}^{k});\quad\bar{b}_{L}^{k}={\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})-J^{k})\phi(s^{(0)})\right].

For
I_{12}
, recall from Eq. (
15
) that,

\displaystyle\xi_{t,L}^{k}=\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}+(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}.

(40)

Then,

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}\xi_{t,L}^{k}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}+(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\big]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}(\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k})\right\rangle+2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t}^{k}-x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}(\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k})\right\rangle}_{I_{121}}+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau})(\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k})\right\rangle}_{I_{122}}+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}(\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k})\right\rangle}_{I_{123}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t}^{k}-x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right\rangle}_{I_{124}}+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau})(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right\rangle}_{I_{125}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k})({\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})\right\rangle}_{I_{126}}+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\right\rangle}_{I_{127}}.

For
I_{121}
, we have under conditional expectation,

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t}^{k}-x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}\big(\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}-x_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t}\|\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+2\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+2\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+U_{\omega}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+2\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|^{2}.

(41)

Similarly, for
I_{122}
, we have

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau})\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big]\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}\|\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|~~~~(\text{by Cauchy Schwartz})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|^{2}.

(42)

For
I_{124}
,

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t}^{k}-x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+2U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+U_{\omega}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+2U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|^{2}.

(43)

For
I_{125}
,

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau})(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8\frac{\beta}{L}U_{\omega}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|^{2}.

(44)

For
I_{126}
, we have,

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k})({\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\frac{\beta}{L}U_{\omega}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+U_{\omega}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}

(45)

Summing up the upper bounds of
I_{121}
(Eq. (
D.1
))
,I_{122}
(Eq. (
D.1
))
,I_{124}
(Eq. (
D.1
)),
I_{125}
(Eq. (
D.1
)) and
I_{126}
(Eq. (
D.1
)), conditioning on
v_{t-\tau}
, we get,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[I_{121}+I_{122}+I_{124}+I_{125}+I_{126}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(1+3U_{\omega}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(10U_{\omega}^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{4}\right)\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(2+32U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(1+3U_{\omega}\right)\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(10U_{\omega}^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{4}\right)\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(2+32U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|^{2}.

(46)

For
I_{123}
, by Assumption
2
and Lemma
I.6
, we have

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big]\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big]\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\beta}{L}\|x_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}\|\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big]\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\beta}{L}2U_{\omega}4LU_{r}m\rho^{\tau}=16U_{\omega}U_{r}\beta m\rho^{\tau}.

For
I_{127}
, similarly, by Assumption
2
and Lemma
I.6
, we have

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\right\rangle\leq 16U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\beta}{L}m\rho^{\tau}.

Then, by the tower property, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{123}+I_{127}]\leq 16U_{\omega}(U_{r}+\frac{U_{\omega}}{L})\beta m\rho^{\tau}\leq 16U_{\omega}(U_{r}+U_{\omega})\beta m\rho^{\tau}.

(47)

Putting Eq. (
47
) and Eq. (
D.1
) together, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{12}]\leq 3\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(1+3U_{\omega}\right)\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(10U_{\omega}^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{4}\right)\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(2+32U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|^{2}+16U_{\omega}(U_{r}+U_{\omega})\beta m\rho^{\tau}.

(48)

Invoking Assumption
1
, putting Eq. (

), Eq. (
D.1
), and Eq. (

), back to Eq. (

), and taking conditional expectation on

, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{1}]=2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\delta_{t,L}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-2\lambda\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+12U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|+2\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\||y_{t}^{k}|

\displaystyle+3\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(1+3U_{\omega}\right)\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(10U_{\omega}^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{4}\right)\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(2+32U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|^{2}+16U_{\omega}(U_{r}+U_{\omega})\beta m\rho^{\tau}.

(49)

Apply Young’s inequality, for arbitrary
c_{3}>0
,
c_{4}>0
, we have

\displaystyle 12U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|\leq c_{3}^{2}\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{36U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{3}^{2}}\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|m_{t}\|^{2},

\displaystyle 2\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\||y_{t}^{k}|\leq c_{4}^{2}\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{c_{4}^{2}}\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2},

we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[I_{1}]\leq-2\lambda\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+c_{3}^{2}\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{36U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{3}^{2}}\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|m_{t}\|^{2}+c_{4}^{2}\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{c_{4}^{2}}\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}

\displaystyle+3\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(1+3U_{\omega}\right)\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(10U_{\omega}^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{4}\right)\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(2+32U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|^{2}+16U_{\omega}(U_{r}+U_{\omega})\beta m\rho^{\tau}.

(50)

Bounding
I_{2}
. By Proposition
1
,
I_{2}
can be written as,

\displaystyle\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\langle x_{t}^{k},\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})\delta_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\langle x_{t}^{k},\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{i}x_{t}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle+\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\langle x_{t}^{k},\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle+\frac{2\zeta}{K}\langle x_{t}^{k},\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}y_{t}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\|x_{t}^{k}\|\|x_{t}^{i}\|+\underbrace{\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\langle x_{t}^{k},\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle}_{I_{21}}+\frac{2U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\|x_{t}^{k}\||y_{t}^{i}|.

(51)

For
I_{21}
, similar to the derivations of the upper bounds of terms from
I_{121}
to
I_{126}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\langle x_{t}^{k}-x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle+\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}){\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle+\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{i})^{\top}(\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{3}{2}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+\left(18U_{\omega}^{4}+32U_{\omega}^{6}\right)\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\|\omega_{t}^{i}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\rangle.

(52)

For the last term in Eq. (
D.1
), by Cauchy–Schwarz inequality and Lemma
I.6
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}2U_{\omega}^{3}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}]\|

\displaystyle\leq 8\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}^{3}\zeta m\rho^{\tau}.

(53)

Then, putting Eq. (
D.1
) back to Eq. (
D.1
) and then putting the result back to Eq. (
D.1
) and using tower property, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{2}]\leq 4U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|\|x_{t}^{i}\|+2U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\||y_{t}^{i}|+\frac{3}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+\left(18U_{\omega}^{4}+32U_{\omega}^{6}\right)\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{i}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+8\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}^{3}\zeta m\rho^{\tau}.

Applying Young’s inequality, for arbitrary
c_{5}>0
and

\displaystyle 4U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|\|x_{t}^{i}\|\leq c_{5}^{2}\frac{\zeta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{4}}{c_{5}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{i}\|^{2},

\displaystyle 2U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\||y_{t}^{i}|\leq c_{6}^{2}\zeta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{U_{\omega}^{4}}{c_{6}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{i}|^{2}.

We get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{2}]\leq c_{5}^{2}\frac{\zeta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{4}}{c_{5}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{i}\|^{2}+c_{6}^{2}\zeta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{U_{\omega}^{4}}{c_{6}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{i}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{3}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+\left(18U_{\omega}^{4}+32U_{\omega}^{6}\right)\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{i}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{i}\|^{2}+8\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}^{3}\zeta m\rho^{\tau}.

(55)

For
I_{3}
, by Proposition
1
, we have

\displaystyle I_{3}=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left\langle x_{t}^{k},\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\right)\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\right)\right\rangle}_{I_{31}}+\underbrace{\left\langle x_{t}^{k},\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\right)\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}Ly_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right)\right\rangle}_{I_{32}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left\langle x_{t}^{k},\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\right)\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\right)\right\rangle}_{I_{33}}.

By Lemma

,

\displaystyle I_{31}

\displaystyle\leq 2U_{\delta,L}U_{\omega}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2},

(56)

\displaystyle I_{32}

\displaystyle\leq LU_{\delta,L}U_{\omega}\|x_{t}^{k}\||y_{t}^{k}|.

For
I_{33}
, we have,

\displaystyle I_{33}=

\displaystyle\left\langle x_{t}^{k},\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{i}x_{t}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\right)\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\right)\right\rangle+\left\langle x_{t}^{k},\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}Ly_{t}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\right)\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle x_{t}^{k},\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\right)\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2U_{\delta,L}U_{\omega}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\|x_{t}^{k}\|\|x_{t}^{i}\|+LU_{\delta,L}U_{\omega}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\|x_{t}^{k}\||y_{t}^{i}|+\left\langle x_{t}^{k},\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\right)\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\right)\right\rangle.

(58)

For the last term, we have,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t}^{k},\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\right)\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}(x_{t}^{k}-x_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}(x_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp})\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}(x_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{i})^{\top}\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}(x_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{i})^{\top}\left(({\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau})^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}(x_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{i})^{\top}\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\right)~~~~~(\text{bridging})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|U_{\delta,L}^{2}U_{\omega}+7U_{\delta,L}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|+2U_{\omega}U_{\delta,L}^{2}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{i}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{i}\|

\displaystyle+2U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}\frac{1}{K}+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}(x_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{i})^{\top}\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\tau U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{3}}{L}\beta+14\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{3}}{L}U_{\omega}^{3}\tau\zeta+28\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{4}\tau\zeta^{2}+2U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}\frac{1}{K}+8U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau},

where the last inequality follows from (a) the independence across agents, (b) Assumption
2
, and (c) Lemma
I.1
.

Putting this bound back to
I_{33}
(Eq. (
D.1
)), and taking conditional expectation, we get,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{33}]\leq 2U_{\delta,L}U_{\omega}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|\|x_{t}^{i}\|+LU_{\delta,L}U_{\omega}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\||y_{t}^{i}|

\displaystyle+3\tau U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{3}}{L}\beta+14\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{3}}{L}U_{\omega}^{3}\tau\zeta+28\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{4}\tau\zeta^{2}+2U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}\frac{1}{K}+8U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}.

(59)

Putting
I_{31}
(Eq. (

)),
I_{32}
(Eq. (

)), and
I_{33}
(Eq. (
D.1
)) back to
I_{3}
, and taking expectation, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{3}]\leq 2\frac{\zeta\beta}{L^{2}}\bigg(2U_{\delta,L}U_{\omega}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+LU_{\delta,L}U_{\omega}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\||y_{t}^{k}|

\displaystyle\qquad+2U_{\delta,L}U_{\omega}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|\|x_{t}^{i}\|+LU_{\delta,L}U_{\omega}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\||y_{t}^{i}|

\displaystyle\qquad+3\tau U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{3}}{L}\beta+14\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{3}}{L}U_{\omega}^{3}\tau\zeta+28\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{4}\tau\zeta^{2}+2U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}\frac{1}{K}+8U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+2\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\zeta\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\||y_{t}^{k}|

\displaystyle\qquad+4\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta\beta}{L}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|\|x_{t}^{i}\|+2\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\zeta\beta\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\||y_{t}^{i}|

\displaystyle\qquad+6\tau U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{3}}{L^{3}}\zeta\beta^{2}+28\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{3}}{L^{3}}U_{\omega}^{3}\tau\zeta^{2}\beta+56\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}U_{\omega}^{4}\tau\zeta^{3}\beta+4\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta\beta}{K}+16\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}m^{2}\zeta\beta\rho^{2\tau}

\displaystyle\underbrace{(6\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}+2\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega})}_{:=C_{31}}\zeta\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\underbrace{\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}}_{:=C_{32}}\zeta\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}

For

\displaystyle+9U_{\omega}^{4}U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+3\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}U_{\omega}^{2}\beta^{2}\zeta^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{9}{2}\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(\frac{5}{2}+3U_{\omega}\right)\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2K}\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{i}-\omega_{j}^{i}\|^{2}.

, Lemma

, Lemma

, and using

we further get,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}\leq\left(1-2\lambda c\zeta+\left(c_{3}^{2}\frac{c\zeta}{L}+c_{4}^{2}c\zeta+c_{5}^{2}\frac{\zeta}{L}+c_{6}^{2}\zeta+C_{31}c\zeta^{2}+(36+40\tau^{2})\frac{c^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\right)\right){\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{4U_{\omega}^{4}}{c_{5}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{L}+C_{33}\frac{c\zeta^{2}}{L}+\left(36U_{\omega}^{4}+\left(\frac{5}{2}+3U_{\omega}\right)C_{18}\right)\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\right)\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{39}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+C_{41}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+C_{42}\zeta\rho^{\tau}+C_{43}(\tau)\zeta^{3}+C_{44}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{2}\rho^{2\tau}+C_{40}(\tau^{4})\zeta^{4}+C_{45}(\tau^{4})\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2},

Putting Eq. (

) and Eq. (
D.1
), back to Eq. (

), and by tower property, we get,

\displaystyle+2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}\rangle

For

\displaystyle\left\|{\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}}\right\|_{2}\leq\|\frac{\beta}{L}\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\|\leq\beta U_{\delta},~~~~(\text{ since }\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}\leq U_{\delta})

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}+96\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+24\frac{\beta^{2}}{L}U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})+24\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}+100U_{\omega}^{6}U_{\delta}^{4}\zeta^{4}

\displaystyle+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|m_{t+1}\|^{2}+\frac{256U_{\omega}^{4}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{i}\|^{2}+64\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+128U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\zeta^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}

\displaystyle+144U_{\omega}^{2}\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}+\frac{64U_{\omega}^{4}\zeta^{2}}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{i}|^{2}.

(69)

Recall
M_{t}={\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2};\bar{X}_{t}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2};\bar{Y}_{t}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}.

Invoking Eq. (
D.1
), and taking average over agents, Eq. (
D.1
) can be written as,

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Bigg(1-2\lambda c\zeta+\Big(c_{3}^{2}\frac{c}{L}+c_{4}^{2}c+c_{5}^{2}\frac{1}{L}+c_{6}^{2}+4U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{1}{c_{5}^{2}L}\Big)\zeta

\displaystyle+\bigg(C_{31}c+(36+40\tau^{2})\frac{c^{2}}{L^{2}}+C_{33}\frac{c}{L}+\left(36U_{\omega}^{4}+\left(2.5+3U_{\omega}\right)C_{18}\right)\frac{1}{L^{2}}+\frac{96c^{2}}{L^{2}}+\frac{256U_{\omega}^{4}}{L^{2}}\bigg)\zeta^{2}\Bigg)\bar{X}_{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{36U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{3}^{2}}\frac{c\zeta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|m_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\Bigg(\Big[\frac{1}{c_{4}^{2}}c+\frac{U_{\omega}^{4}}{c_{6}^{2}}\Big]\zeta+\Big[C_{32}c+(9+10\tau^{2})c^{2}+C_{34}c+9U_{\omega}^{4}+\left(2.5+3U_{\omega}\right)C_{23}+24c^{2}+64U_{\omega}^{4}\big]\zeta^{2}\Bigg)\bar{Y}_{t}

\displaystyle+\left[C_{39}(\tau^{2})+24c^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})\right]\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+\left[C_{41}(\tau^{2})+64\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{4}\right]\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}

\displaystyle+C_{42}\zeta\rho^{\tau}+C_{43}(\tau)\zeta^{3}+\left[C_{40}(\tau^{4})+100U_{\omega}^{6}U_{\delta}^{4}+144U_{\omega}^{2}\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}c^{2}\right]\zeta^{4}+C_{45}(\tau^{4})\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2}

\displaystyle+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|m_{t+1}\|^{2}+\left[128U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}m^{2}+C_{44}(\tau^{2})\right]\zeta^{2}\rho^{2\tau}.

(70)

By Eq. (
E
), Eq. (
D.1
) can be written as,

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{t+1}\leq

\displaystyle\Bigg(1-2\lambda c\zeta+\Big(c_{3}^{2}\frac{c}{L}+c_{4}^{2}c+c_{5}^{2}\frac{1}{L}+c_{6}^{2}+4U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{1}{c_{5}^{2}L}\Big)\zeta

\displaystyle+\bigg(C_{31}c+(36+40\tau^{2})\frac{c^{2}}{L^{2}}+C_{33}\frac{c}{L}+\left(36U_{\omega}^{4}+\left(2.5+3U_{\omega}\right)C_{18}\right)\frac{1}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{96c^{2}}{L^{2}}+\frac{256U_{\omega}^{4}}{L^{2}}+96U_{\delta}c\frac{U_{\omega}^{3}}{c_{2}^{2}}\frac{1}{L^{2}}\bigg)\zeta^{2}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,4}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{3}\Bigg)\bar{X}_{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{36U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{3}^{2}}\frac{c\zeta}{L}M_{t}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c\zeta M_{t}

\displaystyle+\Bigg(\Big[\frac{1}{c_{4}^{2}}c+\frac{U_{\omega}^{4}}{c_{6}^{2}}\Big]\zeta+\Bigg[C_{32}c+(9+10\tau^{2})c^{2}+C_{34}c+9U_{\omega}^{4}+\left(2.5+3U_{\omega}\right)C_{23}

\displaystyle\qquad+24c^{2}+64U_{\omega}^{4}+24U_{\delta}c\frac{U_{\omega}^{3}}{c_{1}^{2}}\Bigg]\zeta^{2}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,5}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{3}\Bigg)\bar{Y}_{t}

\displaystyle+\left[C_{39}(\tau^{2})+24c^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})\right]\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+\left[C_{41}(\tau^{2})+64\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{4}\right]\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}

\displaystyle+C_{42}\zeta\rho^{\tau}+\left[C_{43}(\tau)+24U_{\omega}U_{\delta}cC_{25}(\tau^{2})\frac{1}{L}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2})\frac{1}{K}\right]\zeta^{3}

\displaystyle+\left[C_{40}(\tau^{4})+100U_{\omega}^{6}U_{\delta}^{4}+144U_{\omega}^{2}\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}c^{2}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,6}\right]\zeta^{4}+C_{45}(\tau^{4})\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2}

\displaystyle+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,1}\zeta^{5}+24U_{\omega}U_{\delta}cC_{28}(\tau^{4})\gamma^{2}\zeta^{3}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,3}\zeta^{7}+\left[128U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}m^{2}+C_{44}(\tau^{2})\right]\zeta^{2}\rho^{2\tau}.

(71)

Choosing
\tau=\lceil 2\log_{\rho}(\zeta)\rceil
, we have

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{t+1}\leq\Bigg(1-2\lambda c\zeta+\Big(c_{3}^{2}\frac{c}{L}+c_{4}^{2}c+c_{5}^{2}\frac{1}{L}+c_{6}^{2}+4U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{1}{c_{5}^{2}L}\Big)\zeta+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,1}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,2}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{3}\Bigg)\bar{X}_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\frac{36U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{3}^{2}}\frac{c\zeta}{L}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c\zeta\right)M_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+\Bigg(\Big[\frac{1}{c_{4}^{2}}c+\frac{U_{\omega}^{4}}{c_{6}^{2}}\Big]\zeta+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,4}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{3}\Bigg)\bar{Y}_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,5}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,6}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,7}\zeta^{3}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,8}\zeta^{4}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,9}\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,10}\zeta^{5}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,11}\gamma^{2}\zeta^{3}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,12}\zeta^{7}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,13}\zeta^{6},

(72)

where

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,1}(\tau^{2})=\bigg(C_{31}c+(36+40\tau^{2})\frac{c^{2}}{L^{2}}+C_{33}\frac{c}{L}+\left(36U_{\omega}^{4}+\left(2.5+3U_{\omega}\right)C_{18}\right)\frac{1}{L^{2}}+\frac{96c^{2}}{L^{2}}+\frac{256U_{\omega}^{4}}{L^{2}}+96U_{\delta}c\frac{U_{\omega}^{3}}{c_{2}^{2}}\frac{1}{L^{2}}\bigg),

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,2}(\tau^{2})=6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,4}(\tau^{2}),

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}(\tau^{2})=\Bigg[C_{32}c+(9+10\tau^{2})c^{2}+C_{34}c+9U_{\omega}^{4}+\left(2.5+3U_{\omega}\right)C_{23}+24c^{2}+64U_{\omega}^{4}+24U_{\delta}c\frac{U_{\omega}^{3}}{c_{1}^{2}}\Bigg],

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,4}(\tau^{2})=6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,5}(\tau^{2}),{\mathcal{C}}_{X,5}(\tau^{2})=\left[C_{39}(\tau^{2})+24c^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})\right],{\mathcal{C}}_{X,6}(\tau^{2})=\left[C_{41}(\tau^{2})+64\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{4}\right],

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,7}=\left[C_{42}+C_{43}(\tau)+24U_{\omega}U_{\delta}cC_{25}(\tau^{2})\frac{1}{L}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2})\frac{1}{K}\right],

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,8}=\left[C_{40}(\tau^{4})+100U_{\omega}^{6}U_{\delta}^{4}+144U_{\omega}^{2}\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}c^{2}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,6}\right],{\mathcal{C}}_{X,9}=C_{45}(\tau^{4}),{\mathcal{C}}_{X,10}=6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,1},

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,11}=24U_{\omega}U_{\delta}cC_{28}(\tau^{4}),{\mathcal{C}}_{X,12}=6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c{\mathcal{C}}_{M,3},{\mathcal{C}}_{X,13}=\left[128U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}m^{2}+C_{44}(\tau^{2})\right].

∎

D.2
