Lower bound: Proof of Lemma
5.2

Recall that
x_{t}^{k}={\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z^{k,*}={\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega^{k,*}
.
By definition, for each
k\in[K]
,
{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}
lies in the subspace spanned by
{\mathbf{B}}_{t}
. As illustrated in Fig.
3
, the distance between
{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}
and
z^{k,*}
is no smaller than the distance

Figure 3
:
Geometry illustration.

between
z^{k,*}
and the subspace spanned by
{\mathbf{B}}_{t}
.
Hence,

\displaystyle\|x_{t}^{k}\|

\displaystyle=\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z^{k,*}\|\geq\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}z^{k,*}\|

\displaystyle=\|({\mathbf{I}}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t})z^{k,*}\|=\|({\mathbf{P}}^{*}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t})z^{k,*}\|

\displaystyle=\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}^{*})z^{k,*}\|.

Recall that
{\mathbf{P}}_{t}={\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}
and
{\mathbf{P}}^{*}={\mathbf{B}}^{*}({\mathbf{B}}^{*})^{\top}
. We first rewrite
{\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}^{*}
to relate it to the cross terms
{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*}
and
{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}
.
Observe that

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\\
{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}\end{bmatrix}({\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}{\mathbf{B}}^{*\top})\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*}&{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*}&\textbf{0}\\
\textbf{0}&-{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\end{bmatrix}.

It is easy to check that

\displaystyle{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}{\mathbf{B}}^{*\top}=\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}&{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*}&\textbf{0}\\
\textbf{0}&-{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\\
{\mathbf{B}}^{*\top}.\end{bmatrix}

We can do singular value decomposition for the diagonal blocks; for ease of exposition, we drop the time index
t
in the decomposition.
That is

\displaystyle{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*}={\mathbf{X}}_{1}\bf\Sigma_{1}{\mathbf{Y}}_{1}^{\top},~~~\text{and}~~~-{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}={\mathbf{X}}_{2}\bf\Sigma_{2}{\mathbf{Y}}_{2}^{\top}.

Then, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}{\mathbf{B}}^{*\top}=\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}&{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{X}}_{1}&\textbf{0}\\
\textbf{0}&{\mathbf{X}}_{2}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bf\Sigma_{1}&\textbf{0}\\
\textbf{0}&\bf\Sigma_{2}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{Y}}_{1}^{\top}&\textbf{0}\\
\textbf{0}&{\mathbf{Y}}_{2}^{\top}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\\
{\mathbf{B}}^{*\top}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{1}&{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{X}}_{2}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bf\Sigma_{1}&\textbf{0}\\
\textbf{0}&\bf\Sigma_{2}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}({\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{Y}}_{1})^{\top}\\
({\mathbf{B}}^{*}{\mathbf{Y}}_{2})^{\top}\end{bmatrix}.

So,

\displaystyle\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}^{*})z^{k,*}\|=\left\|{\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{X}}_{1}&{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{X}}_{2}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\bf\Sigma_{1}&\textbf{0}\\
\textbf{0}&\bf\Sigma_{2}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}({\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{Y}}_{1})^{\top}\\
({\mathbf{B}}^{*}{\mathbf{Y}}_{2})^{\top}\end{bmatrix}z^{k,*}}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|{\begin{bmatrix}\bf\Sigma_{1}&\textbf{0}\\
\textbf{0}&\bf\Sigma_{2}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}({\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{Y}}_{1})^{\top}\\
({\mathbf{B}}^{*}{\mathbf{Y}}_{2})^{\top}\end{bmatrix}z^{k,*}}\right\|

From the proof of
(
7
, Lemma 2.4)
, we know that when
d\geq 2r
,
\bf\Sigma_{1}
and
\bf\Sigma_{2}
are identical up to permutation.
Let
\sigma_{1}
denote the largest singular value.
From
(
7
, Lemma 2.5)
, we know that

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}=\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*}\|=\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\|=\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}{\mathbf{B}}^{*\top}\|=\|m_{t}\|.

(73)

Let
{\mathbf{v}}_{1}
and
{\mathbf{v}}_{1}^{*}
denote the corresponding right singular vectors of
{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*}
and
{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}
.
Let
\widetilde{{\mathbf{v}}}_{1}=B^{*}{\mathbf{v}}_{1}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
, and
\widetilde{{\mathbf{v}}}^{*}_{1}=B^{*}_{\perp}{\mathbf{v}}_{1}^{*}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
. It is easy to see that
\widetilde{{\mathbf{v}}}_{1}\in\text{span}({\mathbf{B}}^{*})
and
\widetilde{{\mathbf{v}}}_{1}^{*}\in\text{span}({\mathbf{B}}^{*}_{\perp})
.
Hence, we get

\displaystyle\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}^{*})z^{k,*}\|=

\displaystyle\left\|{\begin{bmatrix}\bf\Sigma_{1}&\textbf{0}\\
\textbf{0}&\bf\Sigma_{2}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}({\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{Y}}_{1})^{\top}\\
({\mathbf{B}}^{*}{\mathbf{Y}}_{2})^{\top}\end{bmatrix}z^{k,*}}\right\|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sqrt{(\sigma_{1}\widetilde{{\mathbf{v}}}_{1}^{\top}z^{k,*})^{2}+(\sigma_{1}(\widetilde{{\mathbf{v}}}_{1}^{*})^{\top}z^{k,*})^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}|\widetilde{{\mathbf{v}}}_{1}^{\top}z^{k,*}|,

where the last line follows from
z^{k,*}\in\text{span}({\mathbf{B}}^{*})
and thus
(\widetilde{{\mathbf{v}}}_{1}^{*})^{\top}z^{k,*}=0,\forall k
.

Recall from Eq. (
10
) that
{\mathbf{Z}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d\times K}
stacks
z^{k,*}
as columnes.
Averaging the reconstruction error across all agents, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}\geq\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sigma_{1}^{2}(\widetilde{{\mathbf{v}}}_{1}^{\top}z^{k,*})^{2}=\sigma_{1}^{2}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{{\mathbf{v}}}_{1}^{\top}z^{k,*})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}^{2}\frac{1}{K}\left\|{\widetilde{{\mathbf{v}}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathbf{Z}}^{*}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}^{2}\frac{1}{K}\widetilde{{\mathbf{v}}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathbf{Z}}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}^{*\top}\widetilde{{\mathbf{v}}}_{1}.

Recall from Eq. (
4
) that
z^{k,*}={\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega^{k,*}
.
Then,

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma_{1}^{2}}{K}\widetilde{{\mathbf{v}}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathbf{Z}}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}^{*\top}\widetilde{{\mathbf{v}}}_{1}\geq\frac{\sigma_{1}^{2}}{K}\lambda^{+}_{\min}({\mathbf{Z}}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}^{*\top}).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}\geq\frac{\sigma_{1}^{2}}{K}\lambda^{+}_{\min}({\mathbf{Z}}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}^{*\top})=\frac{\|m_{t}\|^{2}}{K}\lambda^{+}_{\min}({\mathbf{Z}}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}^{*\top}),

where the last equality follows from Eq. (
73
).

Appendix E
Proof of Lemma
5.3
(Bounds on Principal Angle Distance)

Recall from Algorithm
1
of the update of
\bar{\mathbf{B}}
that

\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}=\mathbf{B}_{t}+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}.

Since
\mathbf{B}_{t+1}=\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\mathbf{R}_{t+1}^{-1}
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\mathbf{B}_{t+1}\|^{2}_{F}=\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}_{F}\leq\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\|^{2}_{F}\|{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}.

That is,

\displaystyle\|m_{t+1}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\|\bar{m}_{t+1}\|^{2}_{F}\|{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}.

For
\|\bar{m}_{t+1}\|^{2}_{F}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\bar{m}_{t+1}\|^{2}_{F}=\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\|^{2}_{F}=\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+2\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\mathbf{B}_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle+\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+\underbrace{\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}}_{I.1}+2\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\mathbf{B}_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\mathbf{B}_{t},\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}y_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle}_{I.2}+\underbrace{2\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\mathbf{B}_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle}_{I.3},

(74)

where the last equality follows from Proposition
1
.

For I.1 in Eq. (
E
), recalling that
\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}=\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle I.1=

\displaystyle\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\|^{2}\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}.

Similar to the derivation in the proof of Lemma
5.1
, I.2 in Eq. (
E
) can be bounded as

\displaystyle I.2=2\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}y_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle\leq 2\|m_{t}\|\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}y_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|\leq 2U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\||y_{t}^{k}|.

I.3
in Eq. (
E
) is related to the negative drift of the
m_{t}
. Specifically,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}I.3=

\displaystyle\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\mathbf{B}_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{P}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)^{\top}A_{L}^{k}\left({\mathbf{P}}_{t}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\right)x_{t}^{k},

which is true because

\left\langle AB,vw^{\top}\right\rangle=\text{trace}\left(A^{\top}vw^{\top}\right)=w^{\top}B^{\top}A^{\top}v

for any
r\times r
matrix
A
and
r
-dimensional vectors
v
and
w
.
Additionally,

\displaystyle\left({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{P}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)^{\top}A_{L}^{k}\left({\mathbf{P}}_{t}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\right)x_{t}^{k}

\displaystyle\leq\left({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{P}}_{\perp}^{*}x_{t}^{k}\right)^{\top}A_{L}^{k}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}x_{t}^{k}+2U_{\omega}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq\left({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}x_{t}^{k}\right)^{\top}A_{L}^{k}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}x_{t}^{k}+6U_{\omega}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq-\lambda L\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+6U_{\omega}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

For each
k\in[K]
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}=\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}(\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega^{k,*})\|^{2}=\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}z^{k,*}\|=\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}^{*})z^{k,*}\|.

From the analysis in the proof of Lemma
5.2
, we know that

\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}^{*})z^{k,*}\|^{2}\geq\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}\frac{\lambda_{\min}({\mathbf{Z}}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}^{*\top})}{rK}.

So,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{\perp}^{*}(\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega^{k,*})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}\frac{\lambda_{\min}({\mathbf{Z}}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}^{*\top})}{rK}.

Thus,

\displaystyle I.3\leq-2\lambda\zeta\frac{\lambda_{\min}({\mathbf{Z}}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}^{*})^{\top}}{rK}\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+12U_{\omega}\zeta\frac{1}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

(75)

Combining the bounds of I.1, I.2, and I.3 of Eq. (
E
), and applying Assumption
4
, we have

\displaystyle\|\bar{m}_{t+1}\|_{F}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{2\lambda\alpha\zeta}{r})\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+2\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+2U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\||y_{t}^{k}|+12U_{\omega}\zeta\frac{1}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

(76)

Recall that
\|m_{t+1}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\|\bar{m}_{t+1}\|^{2}_{F}\|{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}
,
we have

\displaystyle\|m_{t+1}\|_{F}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{2\lambda\alpha\zeta}{r})\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}\|{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}\|{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}+2\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle\|{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+2U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\||y_{t}^{k}|\|{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}+12U_{\omega}\zeta\frac{1}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|\|{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}.

For the first term, we use Eq. (
21
) that
{\|{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}^{-1}\|^{2}\leq(\frac{1}{1-U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}})^{2}}
, and for the remaining terms, we use a crude bound that
\|{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}^{-1}\|^{2}\leq(\frac{1}{1-U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}})^{2}\leq 2
when
U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}\leq\frac{1}{6}
, then,

\displaystyle\|m_{t+1}\|_{F}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{r})(\frac{1}{1-U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}})^{2}\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+4\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+4U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\||y_{t}^{k}|+24U_{\omega}\zeta\frac{1}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

Moreover, since
U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}\leq\frac{1}{6}
, we have
(\frac{1}{1-U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}})^{2}\leq 1+3U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}
.
Providing
\frac{\lambda\alpha}{r}\geq 3U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta
, it holds that

\displaystyle(1-\frac{2\lambda\alpha\zeta}{r})(\frac{1}{1-U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}})^{2}\leq(1-\frac{2\lambda\alpha\zeta}{r})(1+3U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2})\leq(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{r}).

Then,

\displaystyle\|m_{t+1}\|_{F}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{r})\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+4\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+4U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\||y_{t}^{k}|+24U_{\omega}\zeta\frac{1}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

(77)

By Young’s inequality, we have for any
c_{1}>0,c_{2}>0
,

\displaystyle 4U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\||y_{t}^{k}|\leq c_{1}^{2}\zeta\|m_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{1}^{2}}\zeta\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2},

\displaystyle 16U_{\omega}\zeta\frac{1}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|\leq c_{2}^{2}\zeta\|m_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{144U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{2}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{L^{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}.

Putting this back to Eq. (
E
), we get,

\displaystyle\|m_{t+1}\|_{F}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{r}+c_{1}^{2}\zeta+c_{2}^{2}\zeta)\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+4\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{1}^{2}}\zeta\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}+\frac{144U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{2}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{L^{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}.

(78)

For the third term of Eq. (
E
), we have

\displaystyle\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}+(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\big](\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle~~~~~(\text{by Eq.\,(\ref{eqn: xi decomp})})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big](\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle+\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle m_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big](\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle+\langle m_{t}-m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big](\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp})\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big](\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle+\underbrace{\langle m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big](\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle}_{I_{1}}

\displaystyle+\langle m_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k})\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\right)\omega_{t}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle m_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}(\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle m_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle m_{t}-m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\left({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}\right)\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\langle m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle}_{I_{2}}.

(79)

Note that
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
in the above decomposition can be bounded under expectation conditioned on
v_{0:t-\tau}
, and other terms are in terms of
\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}
,
\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}
,
\left\|{\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}}\right\|^{2}
, and
\left\|{\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}}\right\|^{2}
by applying Cauchy-Schwartz. Specifically, for the first term, we have

\displaystyle\langle m_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big](\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left\|{\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}}\right\|\left\|{\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\frac{\zeta}{2L}\left\|{\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{L}{2\zeta}\left\|{\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left\|{\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left\|{\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}}\right\|^{2}.

Applying similar analysis to all other terms in Eq. (
E
), we obtain

\displaystyle\langle m_{t}-m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big](\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+\frac{U_{\omega}^{2}}{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left\|{\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}}\right\|^{2},

\displaystyle\langle m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp})\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big](\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle\leq 2\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left\|{\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}}\right\|^{2},

\displaystyle\langle m_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k})\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\right)\omega_{t}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle\leq\frac{1}{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2},

\displaystyle\langle m_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}(\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq\frac{U_{\omega}}{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{U_{\omega}}{2}\!\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2},

\displaystyle\langle m_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq\frac{U_{\omega}}{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{U_{\omega}}{2}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2},

\displaystyle\langle m_{t}-m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq\frac{1}{2}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|^{2},

\displaystyle\langle m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\left({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\right)\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq 2\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+\frac{U_{\omega}^{4}}{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|^{2}

Combining the above bounds and pulgging them back to Eq. (
E
), and taking expectation conditioned on
v_{0:t-\tau}
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\|{\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\|{\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(U_{\omega}+\frac{3}{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\right)\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|^{2}+8{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\langle m_{t-\tau},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big](\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle}_{I_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\langle m_{t-\tau},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}]{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle}_{I_{2}}.

(80)

Note that Lemma
I.5
holds for any
t\geq 0
and any state
s_{t,k}^{0}
. Applying Lemma
I.5
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\|{\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}}\right\|^{2}\leq\left(16U_{r}^{2}+32U_{r}^{2}\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho}\right)L.

(81)

Similarly, we have,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\|^{2}\leq\left(16+32\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho}\right)L.~~~~(\text{ Lemma \ref{lmm: local noise reduction}, Eq.\,(\ref{eqn: Atilde-Abar ^2 local noise})})

(82)

Applying Lemma
I.2
and Lemma
I.3
, we have the following,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tau^{2}C_{5}(\tau^{2})\frac{\beta^{4}}{L^{2}}+\tau^{2}C_{6}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}+8\tau^{2}\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\tau^{2}C_{7}\frac{\beta^{2}}{L}+\tau^{2}C_{8}(\tau^{2})\gamma^{2}\beta^{2}+2\tau^{2}\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2},

(83)

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tau^{2}C_{16}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}+\tau^{2}C_{17}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{4}}{L^{2}}+\tau^{2}C_{18}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\tau^{2}C_{19}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}+\tau^{2}C_{20}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}

\displaystyle+\tau^{2}C_{21}\zeta^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}+\tau^{2}C_{22}(\tau^{2})\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2}+\tau^{2}C_{23}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}+\tau^{2}C_{24}\zeta^{4}.

(84)

For
I_{1}
, by Lemma
I.6
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{1}]={\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\langle m_{t-\tau},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big](\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle m_{t-\tau},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big](\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4U_{r}U_{\omega}\zeta m\rho^{\tau}.

(85)

Similarly, for
I_{2}
, by Lemma
I.6
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{2}]={\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\langle m_{t-\tau},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\langle m_{t-\tau},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}\right]{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta}{L}m\rho^{\tau}.

(86)

Putting Eq. (
81
), Eq. (
82
), Eq. (
E
), Eq. (
E
), Eq. (
E
) and Eq. (
E
) back to Eq. (
E
), taking conditional expectation, and using
\beta=c\zeta
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\left(16U_{r}^{2}+32U_{r}^{2}\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho}\right)\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)\bigg(\tau^{2}C_{5}(\tau^{2})c^{4}\frac{\zeta^{4}}{L^{2}}+\tau^{2}C_{6}(\tau^{2})c^{2}\frac{\zeta^{4}}{L^{2}}+8\tau^{2}c^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\tau^{2}C_{7}c^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+\tau^{2}C_{8}(\tau^{2})c^{2}\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2}+2\tau^{2}c^{2}\zeta^{2}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\left(U_{\omega}+\frac{3}{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\right)\left(16+32\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho}\right)\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}

\displaystyle+8\bigg(\tau^{2}C_{16}(\tau^{2})c^{2}\frac{\zeta^{4}}{L^{2}}+\tau^{2}C_{17}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{4}}{L^{2}}+\tau^{2}C_{18}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\tau^{2}C_{19}(\tau^{2})c^{2}\zeta^{4}+\tau^{2}C_{20}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\tau^{2}C_{21}\zeta^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}+\tau^{2}C_{22}(\tau^{2})\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2}+\tau^{2}C_{23}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}+\tau^{2}C_{24}\zeta^{4}\bigg)

\displaystyle+2U_{r}U_{\omega}\zeta m\rho^{\tau}+2U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta}{L}m\rho^{\tau}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{25}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+C_{26}(\tau^{4})\frac{\zeta^{4}}{L^{2}}+C_{27}(\tau^{4})\zeta^{4}+C_{28}(\tau^{4})\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{29}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+C_{30}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}

\displaystyle+8\tau^{2}C_{20}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+8\tau^{2}C_{21}\zeta^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}+2U_{r}U_{\omega}\zeta m\rho^{\tau}+2U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta}{L}m\rho^{\tau}.

(87)

where

\displaystyle C_{25}(\tau^{2})=\Bigg[\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\left(16U_{r}^{2}+32U_{r}^{2}\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho}\right)+\left(U_{\omega}+\frac{3}{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\right)\left(16+32\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho}\right)+\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)\tau^{2}C_{7}c^{2}\Bigg],

\displaystyle C_{26}(\tau^{4})=\Bigg[\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)\left(\tau^{2}C_{5}(\tau^{2})c^{4}+\tau^{2}C_{6}(\tau^{2})c^{2}\right)+8\left(\tau^{2}C_{16}(\tau^{2})c^{2}+\tau^{2}C_{17}(\tau^{2})\right)\Bigg],

\displaystyle C_{27}(\tau^{4})=\Bigg[8\tau^{2}C_{19}(\tau^{2})c^{2}+8\tau^{2}C_{24}\Bigg],C_{28}(\tau^{4})=\Bigg[\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)\tau^{2}C_{8}(\tau^{2})c^{2}+8\tau^{2}C_{22}(\tau^{2})\Bigg],

\displaystyle C_{29}(\tau^{2})=\Bigg[8\tau^{2}c^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)+8\tau^{2}C_{18}\Bigg],C_{30}(\tau^{2})=\Bigg[2\tau^{2}c^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)+8\tau^{2}C_{23}\Bigg].

Putting the above Eq. (
E
) and Eq. (
C.2
) back to Eq. (
E
) and taking expectation, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|m_{t+1}\|_{F}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{r}+c_{1}^{2}\zeta+c_{2}^{2}\zeta){\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{32U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}+4C_{29}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+\frac{144U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{2}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{L^{2}}\right)\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(4C_{30}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{2}+8U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{1}^{2}}\zeta\right)\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}

\displaystyle+4C_{25}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+4C_{26}(\tau^{4})\frac{\zeta^{4}}{L^{2}}+\left(4C_{27}(\tau^{4})+8\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}c^{2}\right)\zeta^{4}+4C_{28}(\tau^{4})\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left(32\tau^{2}C_{20}+8\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+\left(32\tau^{2}C_{21}+16U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\right)\zeta^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}+8U_{r}U_{\omega}\zeta m\rho^{\tau}+8U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta}{L}m\rho^{\tau}.

(88)

Recall
M_{t}={\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2};\bar{X}_{t}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2};\bar{Y}_{t}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2},
and choose
\tau=\lceil 2\log_{\rho}(\zeta)\rceil
, we get,

\displaystyle M_{t+1}\leq(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{r}+c_{1}^{2}\zeta+c_{2}^{2}\zeta)M_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left({\mathcal{C}}_{M,4}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{2}+\frac{144U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{2}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{L^{2}}+4\lambda\zeta\right)\bar{X}_{t}+\left({\mathcal{C}}_{M,5}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{2}+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{1}^{2}}\zeta\right)\bar{Y}_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+4C_{25}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+{\mathcal{C}}_{M,1}\zeta^{4}+4C_{28}(\tau^{4})\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{C}}_{M,3}\zeta^{6}+{\mathcal{C}}_{M,6}\zeta^{3},

(89)

where

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{M,1}=\left(4C_{27}(\tau^{4})+8\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}c^{2}+4C_{26}(\tau^{4})\frac{1}{L^{2}}\right),{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2})=\left(32\tau^{2}C_{20}+8\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}\right),

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{M,3}=\left(32\tau^{2}C_{21}+16U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\right)m^{2},{\mathcal{C}}_{M,4}(\tau^{2})=\left[\frac{32U_{\omega}^{2}}{L^{2}}+\frac{4C_{29}(\tau^{2})}{L}\right],{\mathcal{C}}_{M,5}(\tau^{2})=\left[4C_{30}(\tau^{2})+8U_{\omega}^{2}\right],

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{M,6}=\left[8U_{r}U_{\omega}m+8U_{\omega}^{2}m\frac{1}{L}\right].

Appendix F
Proof of Lemma
5.4

By Eq. (
11
) and the update of
\eta^{k}
in Algorithm
1
, we have

\displaystyle y_{t+1}^{k}=

\displaystyle\eta_{t+1}^{k}-J^{k}=\eta_{t}^{k}+\gamma\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{t,\ell}^{k}-\eta_{t}^{k}\right)-J^{k}=y_{t}^{k}+\gamma\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{t,\ell}^{k}-\eta_{t}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle y_{t}^{k}+\gamma\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}+J^{k}-\eta_{t}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle y_{t}^{k}+\gamma\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}-y_{t}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\gamma)y_{t}^{k}+\gamma\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\gamma)^{t+1}y_{0}^{k}+\gamma\sum_{i=0}^{t}(1-\gamma)^{t-i}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right).

Then,

\displaystyle(y_{t}^{k})^{2}=

\displaystyle(1-\gamma)^{2t}(y_{0}^{k})^{2}+\gamma^{2}\left(\sum_{i=0}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+2(1-\gamma)^{t}y_{0}^{k}\gamma\sum_{i=0}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right).

Taking expectation, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}(y_{t}^{k})^{2}=

\displaystyle\underbrace{(1-\gamma)^{2t}(y_{0}^{k})^{2}}_{I_{1}}+\underbrace{\gamma^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left(\sum_{i=0}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)\right)^{2}}_{I_{2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2(1-\gamma)^{t}y_{0}^{k}\gamma{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=0}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)}_{I_{3}}.

(90)

For
I_{1}
in Eq. (
F
), using the fact that
\ln(1-\gamma)\leq-\gamma
for
\gamma\in(0,1)
, we have

\displaystyle I_{1}=(1-\gamma)^{2t}(y_{0}^{k})^{2}\leq(y_{0}^{k})^{2}e^{-2\gamma t}.

For
I_{3}
in Eq. (
F
), we have,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=0}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=0}^{\tau-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)+{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=\tau}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2U_{r}\frac{(1-\gamma)^{t-\tau}(1-(1-\gamma)^{\tau})}{1-(1-\gamma)}+\sum_{i=\tau}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}{\mathbb{E}}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2U_{r}\frac{(1-\gamma)^{t-\tau}}{\gamma}+2U_{r}\sum_{i=\tau}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}\sup_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}d_{TV}(P_{0:i}^{k}(\cdot,\cdot,\cdot,...,\cdot\mid s_{0}^{k}=s),\mu^{k}\otimes(\pi\otimes P^{k})^{L-1}\otimes\pi)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2U_{r}\frac{(1-\gamma)^{t-\tau}}{\gamma}+2U_{r}\sum_{i=\tau}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}m\rho^{i}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2U_{r}\frac{(1-\gamma)^{t-\tau}}{\gamma}+\frac{2U_{r}m\rho^{\tau}}{\gamma}.

where the first inequality follows because
\left|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right|\leq 2U_{r}
,
and the third inequality follows from Assumption
2
.
In the second inequality, we slightly abuse notation to use
P_{0:i}^{k}(\cdot,\cdot,\cdot,...,\cdot\mid s_{0}^{k}=s)
to denote the
L
-block state-action pair at time
t
. It holds because the mixing of the
L
-block is no slower than the original.
Thus,

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq

\displaystyle 2(1-\gamma)^{t}|y_{0}^{k}|\left(2U_{r}(1-\gamma)^{t-\tau}+2U_{r}m\rho^{\tau}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4|y_{0}^{k}|U_{r}(1-\gamma)^{2t-\tau}+4U_{r}(1-\gamma)^{t}|y_{0}^{k}|m\rho^{\tau}.

Lastly, for
I_{2}
in Eq. (
F
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left(\sum_{i=0}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=0}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{2t-2-2i}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+2{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=0}^{t-1}\sum_{j=i+1}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-j}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{j,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1-(1-\gamma)^{2t}}{1-(1-\gamma)^{2}}4U_{r}^{2}+\underbrace{2{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=0}^{t-\tau-1}\sum_{j=i+\tau}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-j}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{j,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)}_{I_{21}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=0}^{t-\tau-1}\sum_{j=i+1}^{i+\tau-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-j}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{j,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)}_{I_{22}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\sum_{j=i+1}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-j}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{j,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)}_{I_{23}}.

(91)

Recall
v_{0:t}^{k}
is the Markovian trajectory at agent
k
:

\displaystyle v_{0:t}^{k}=(s_{0}^{k},a_{0}^{k},s_{1}^{k},a_{1}^{k},...,s_{t-\tau+1}^{k},a_{t-\tau+1}^{k},...,s_{t}^{k},a_{t}^{k},s_{t+1}^{k}).

(92)

For
I_{21}
in Eq. (
F
), we have

\displaystyle I_{21}=

\displaystyle 2\sum_{i=0}^{t-\tau-1}\sum_{j=i+\tau}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-j}{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{i,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{j,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)|v_{0:i}^{k}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{i=0}^{t-\tau-1}\sum_{j=i+\tau}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-j}2U_{r}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{j,\ell}^{k}-J^{k}\right)|v_{0:i}^{k}\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{i=0}^{t-\tau-1}\sum_{j=i+\tau}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-j}4U^{2}_{r}m\rho^{\tau}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8U^{2}_{r}m\rho^{\tau}\sum_{i=0}^{t-\tau-1}\sum_{j=i+\tau}^{t-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-j}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8U^{2}_{r}m\rho^{\tau}\sum_{i=0}^{t-\tau-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}\frac{1-(1-\gamma)^{t-i-\tau}}{\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8U^{2}_{r}m\rho^{\tau}\frac{(1-\gamma)^{\tau}}{\gamma^{2}}.

Noting that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{t-\tau-1}\sum_{j=i+1}^{i+\tau-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{t-\tau-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}\left(\sum_{j=i+1}^{i+\tau-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-j}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{t-\tau-1}(1-\gamma)^{t-1-i}\frac{(1-\gamma)^{t-i-\tau}(1-(1-\gamma)^{\tau})}{\gamma}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(1-(1-\gamma)^{\tau})}{\gamma}\sum_{i=0}^{t-\tau-1}(1-\gamma)^{2t-1-2i-\tau}.

Since
(1-\gamma)^{\tau}\geq 1-\tau\gamma
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{(1-(1-\gamma)^{\tau})}{\gamma}\leq\frac{1-(1-\tau\gamma)}{\gamma}=\tau.

In addition,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{t-\tau-1}(1-\gamma)^{2t-1-2i-\tau}\leq\frac{(1-\gamma)^{\tau+1}}{1-(1-\gamma)^{2}}\leq\frac{(1-\gamma)^{\tau}}{\gamma}.

Thus,
I_{22}
in Eq. (
F
) can be bounded as

\displaystyle I_{22}\leq 8U_{r}^{2}\tau\frac{(1-\gamma)^{\tau}}{\gamma}.

For
I_{23}
in Eq. (
F
), we have

\displaystyle I_{23}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\sum_{j=i+1}^{t-1}4U_{r}^{2}\leq 8U_{r}^{2}\frac{\tau(\tau-1)}{2}.

Now, we can bound
I_{2}
in Eq. (
F
) as

\displaystyle I_{2}\leq\gamma^{2}\left(\frac{1-(1-\gamma)^{2t}}{1-(1-\gamma)^{2}}4U_{r}^{2}+8U^{2}_{r}m\rho^{\tau}\frac{(1-\gamma)^{\tau}}{\gamma^{2}}+8U_{r}^{2}\tau\frac{(1-\gamma)^{\tau}}{\gamma}+8U_{r}^{2}\frac{\tau(\tau-1)}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma}{2-\gamma}4U_{r}^{2}+8U_{r}m\rho^{\tau}(1-\gamma)^{\tau}+8U_{r}^{2}\tau\gamma(1-\gamma)^{\tau}+8U_{r}^{2}\frac{\tau(\tau-1)}{2}\gamma^{2}.

Putting
I_{1},I_{2}
and
I_{3}
together, and choosing
\gamma=\frac{\log(T)}{2T}
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}(y_{T}^{k})^{2}\leq(y_{0}^{k})^{2}e^{-2\gamma T}+\frac{\gamma}{2-\gamma}4U_{r}^{2}+8U^{2}_{r}m\rho^{\tau}(1-\gamma)^{\tau}+8U_{r}^{2}\tau\gamma(1-\gamma)^{\tau}

\displaystyle+8U_{r}^{2}\frac{\tau(\tau-1)}{2}\gamma^{2}+4|y_{0}^{k}|U_{r}e^{\gamma\tau}e^{-\gamma 2T}+4U_{r}(1-\gamma)^{T}|y_{0}^{k}|m\rho^{\tau}

\displaystyle\leq 4U_{r}^{2}\frac{1}{T}+\frac{4U_{r}^{2}\log(T)}{2T}+8U_{r}m\rho^{\tau}+8U_{r}^{2}\tau\frac{\log(T)}{2T}

\displaystyle+8U_{r}^{2}\frac{\tau(\tau-1)}{2}\frac{\log^{2}(T)}{4T^{2}}+8U_{r}^{2}e^{\gamma\tau}\frac{1}{T}+8U_{r}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}m\rho^{\tau}.

When
\tau\leq\min\{\frac{2T}{\log(T)},\lceil\log_{\rho}(\gamma)\rceil,\lceil 2\log_{\rho}(\zeta)\rceil\}
, it holds that
e^{\gamma\tau}\leq 3
and that

8U_{r}^{2}\frac{\tau(\tau-1)}{2}\frac{\log^{2}(T)}{4T^{2}}\leq\frac{2\tau U_{r}^{2}\log(T)}{T}
, and
\rho^{\tau}\leq\gamma
.
Thus, the upper bound of
{\mathbb{E}}(y_{T}^{k})^{2}
can be simplified as

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}(y_{T}^{k})^{2}\leq\frac{28U_{r}^{2}}{T}+\frac{8U_{r}^{2}\tau\log(T)}{T}+16U_{r}m\rho^{\tau}\leq\frac{28U_{r}^{2}}{T}+\frac{8U_{r}^{2}\tau\log(T)}{T}+\frac{8U_{r}m\log(T)}{T},

(93)

which trivially implies that

\displaystyle\bar{Y}_{T}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}Y_{T}^{k}\leq\frac{28U_{r}^{2}}{T}+\frac{8U_{r}^{2}\tau\log(T)}{T}+\frac{8U_{r}m\log(T)}{T}.

Appendix G
Proof of Theorem
1

For ease of expostion, let
\bar{Y}_{T}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}Y_{T}^{k}
.
From Lemma
5.4
(Eq. (
93
)), Lemma
D.1
, Lemma
5.2
, and
Lemma
5.3
(Eq. (
E
)), we get, for
T\geq 2\tau
,

\displaystyle\bar{Y}_{T}\leq\frac{28U_{r}^{2}}{T}+\frac{8U_{r}^{2}\tau\log(T)}{T}+\frac{8U_{r}m\log(T)}{T},

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{T+1}\leq\Bigg(1-2\lambda c\zeta+\Big(c_{3}^{2}\frac{c}{L}+c_{4}^{2}c+c_{5}^{2}\frac{1}{L}+c_{6}^{2}+4U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{1}{c_{5}^{2}L}\Big)\zeta+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,1}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,2}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{3}\Bigg)\bar{X}_{T}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\frac{36U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{3}^{2}}\frac{c\zeta}{L}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c\zeta\right)M_{T}

\displaystyle\qquad+\Bigg(\Big[\frac{1}{c_{4}^{2}}c+\frac{U_{\omega}^{4}}{c_{6}^{2}}\Big]\zeta+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,4}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{3}\Bigg)\bar{Y}_{T}

\displaystyle\qquad+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,5}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,6}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,7}\zeta^{3}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,8}\zeta^{4}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,9}\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,10}\zeta^{5}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,11}\gamma^{2}\zeta^{3}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,12}\zeta^{7}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,13}\zeta^{6},

\displaystyle M_{T+1}\leq(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{r}+c_{1}^{2}\zeta+c_{2}^{2}\zeta)M_{T}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left({\mathcal{C}}_{M,4}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{2}+\frac{16U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{2}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{L^{2}}\right)\bar{X}_{T}+\left({\mathcal{C}}_{M,5}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{2}+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{1}^{2}}\zeta\right)\bar{Y}_{T}

\displaystyle\qquad+4C_{25}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+{\mathcal{C}}_{M,1}\zeta^{4}+4C_{28}(\tau^{4})\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{C}}_{M,3}\zeta^{6}+{\mathcal{C}}_{M,6}\zeta^{3}.

Note that
\bar{Y}_{T}={\mathcal{O}}(\frac{\log(T)}{T})
, and we collect all terms of order
\zeta^{3}
and higher into
{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{3})
, then the system can be simplified to,

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{T+1}\leq\Bigg(1-2\lambda c\zeta+\Big(c_{3}^{2}\frac{c}{L}+c_{4}^{2}c+c_{5}^{2}\frac{1}{L}+c_{6}^{2}+4U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{1}{c_{5}^{2}L}\Big)\zeta+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,1}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,2}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{3}\Bigg)\bar{X}_{T}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\frac{36U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{3}^{2}}\frac{c\zeta}{L}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c\zeta\right)M_{T}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,5}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,6}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{\zeta\log(T)}{T})+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{3}),

\displaystyle M_{T+1}\leq(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{r}+c_{1}^{2}\zeta+c_{2}^{2}\zeta)M_{T}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left({\mathcal{C}}_{M,4}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{2}+\frac{16U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{2}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{L^{2}}\right)+4C_{25}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{3})+{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{\zeta\log(T)}{T}).

Choosing
c_{3}=\sqrt{\frac{\lambda L}{30}},c_{4}=\sqrt{\frac{\lambda}{30}},c_{5}=1,c_{6}=\min\{\sqrt{\frac{1}{L}},\sqrt{\frac{4U_{\omega}^{4}}{L}}\}
, and

\displaystyle c\geq\max\{\frac{30}{\lambda L},\frac{120U_{\omega}^{4}}{\lambda L}\},

(94)

and requiring
\zeta\leq\frac{\lambda c}{6{\mathcal{C}}_{X,1}(\tau^{2})},\zeta^{2}\leq\frac{\lambda c}{6{\mathcal{C}}_{X,2}(\tau^{2})}
,
we get

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{T+1}\leq\left(1-\frac{3}{2}\lambda c\zeta\right)\bar{X}_{T}+\left(\frac{36U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{3}^{2}}\frac{c\zeta}{L}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c\zeta\right)M_{T}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,5}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,6}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{\zeta\log(T)}{T})+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{3}).

We choose
c_{1}=c_{2}=\sqrt{\frac{\lambda\alpha}{4r}}
, which requires
{\mathcal{C}}_{M,4}(\tau^{2})\zeta\leq\frac{16U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{2}^{2}L^{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle M_{T+1}\leq\left(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{2r}\right)M_{T}+\frac{32U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{2}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{L^{2}}\bar{X}_{T}+4C_{25}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{3})+{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{\zeta\log(T)}{T}).

Substituting
c_{2}
and
c_{3}
, we get,

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{T+1}\leq\left(1-\frac{3}{2}\lambda c\zeta\right)\bar{X}_{T}+\left(\frac{1080U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda L^{2}}c\zeta+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c\zeta\right)M_{T}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,5}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,6}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta\frac{\log(T)}{T})+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{3}).

\displaystyle M_{T+1}\leq\left(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{2r}\right)M_{T}+\frac{128rU_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda\alpha}\frac{\zeta}{L^{2}}\bar{X}_{T}+4C_{25}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{3})+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta\frac{\log(T)}{T}).

Defining the weighted Lyapunov function as
{\mathcal{V}}_{T+1}=\bar{X}_{T+1}+\kappa M_{T+1}
, we have,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}_{T+1}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{3}{2}\lambda c\zeta+\kappa\frac{128rU_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda\alpha}\frac{\zeta}{L^{2}}\right)\bar{X}_{T}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,5}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,6}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta\frac{\log(T)}{T})+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{3})

\displaystyle+\kappa\left(\left(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{2r}+\frac{1}{\kappa}\left(\frac{1080U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda L^{2}}c\zeta+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c\zeta\right)\right)M_{T}+4C_{25}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{3})+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta\frac{\log(T)}{T})\right)

For sufficiently large
L
,
c
satisfies Eq. (
94
) and the following condition

\displaystyle\kappa\frac{256rU_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}\alpha L^{2}}\leq c\leq\kappa\frac{\lambda^{2}\alpha L^{2}}{4r\left(1080U_{\omega}^{2}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}\lambda L^{2}\right)}

(95)

such that

\displaystyle\frac{128rU_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda\alpha L^{2}}\kappa\leq\frac{1}{2}\lambda c,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\kappa}\left(\frac{1080U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda L^{2}}c+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c\right)\leq\frac{\lambda\alpha}{4r},

then,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}_{T+1}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\lambda c\zeta\right)\bar{X}_{T}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,5}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,6}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta\frac{\log(T)}{T})+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{3})

\displaystyle+\kappa\left(\left(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{4r}\right)M_{T}+4C_{25}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{3})+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta\frac{\log(T)}{T})\right)

(96)

Choosing
\kappa\geq\frac{2(1080U_{\omega}^{2}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}\lambda L^{2})}{\lambda^{2}L^{2}}=\Theta(1)
,
the upper bound of
c
in Eq. (
95
) satisfies,

\displaystyle\kappa\frac{\lambda^{2}\alpha L^{2}}{4r\left(1080U_{\omega}^{2}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}\lambda L^{2}\right)}\geq\frac{\alpha}{2r},

leaving a room to further constrain
c
to be greater than
\frac{\alpha}{4r}
and choose
L=K
, such that
Eq. (
G
) can be written as,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}_{T+1}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{4r}\right)\bar{X}_{T}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,5}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,6}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta\frac{\log(T)}{T})+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{3})

\displaystyle+\kappa\left(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{4r}\right)M_{T}+4\kappa C_{25}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+\kappa{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+\kappa{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{3})+\kappa{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta\frac{\log(T)}{T})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{4r}\right){\mathcal{V}}_{T}+{\mathcal{C}}_{V}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta\frac{\log(T)}{T})+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{3}),

(97)

where

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{V}(\tau^{2})=\left({\mathcal{C}}_{X,5}(\tau^{2})+{\mathcal{C}}_{X,6}(\tau^{2})+4\kappa C_{25}(\tau^{2})+\kappa{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2})\right).

Unrolling Eq. (
G
), we get

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}_{T+1}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{4r}\right){\mathcal{V}}_{T}+{\mathcal{C}}_{V}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta\frac{\log(T)}{T})+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{3})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{4r}\right)^{T-2\tau+1}{\mathcal{V}}_{2\tau}+\sum_{i=0}^{T-2\tau}\left(1-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta}{4r}\right)^{i}\left[{\mathcal{C}}_{V}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta\frac{\log(T)}{T})+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{3})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp\left\{-\frac{\lambda\alpha\zeta(T-2\tau+1)}{4r}\right\}+\frac{4r}{\lambda\alpha}\left[{\mathcal{C}}_{V}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta}{K}+{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{\log(T)}{T})+{\mathcal{O}}(\zeta^{2})\right].

Choosing
\zeta=\frac{4r}{\sqrt{T}\lambda\alpha}
, we have,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}_{T+1}\leq\exp\left\{-\sqrt{T}/2\right\}+\frac{16r^{2}}{\lambda^{2}\alpha^{2}}{\mathcal{C}}_{V}(\tau^{2})\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}K}+{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{\log(T)}{T}).

Lastly, collecting the sufficient conditions on
c
and
K
, we can always choose

\displaystyle\max\left\{\frac{30}{\lambda K},\frac{120U_{\omega}^{4}}{\lambda K}\right\}\leq\frac{\alpha}{2r};\quad\frac{\alpha}{4r}\leq c\leq\frac{\alpha}{2r}.

Appendix H
Supporting Propositions and Lemmas

H.1
Boundedness

Lemma H.1
.

For any
t\geq 0,L\geq 0,k\in[K]
, it holds that
\eta_{t}^{k}\in[-U_{r},U_{r}]
.

Proof.

We prove Lemma
H.1
by induction.
The algorithm initializes
\eta_{0}^{k}=0
.
Suppose that
\eta_{t^{\prime}}^{k}\in[-U_{r},U_{r}]
for
0\leq t^{\prime}\leq t-1
and for any
k\in[K]
.
We have

\displaystyle\eta_{t}^{k}=\eta_{t-1}^{k}+\gamma\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{t-1,\ell}^{k}-\eta^{k}_{t-1}\right)=(1-\gamma)\eta_{t-1}^{k}+\frac{\gamma}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{t-1,\ell}^{k}\leq(1-\gamma)U_{r}+\frac{\gamma}{L}L\cdot U_{r}=U_{r}.

Similarly, it can be shown that
\eta_{t}^{k}\geq-U_{r}
.

∎

Lemma H.2
.

Suppose that Assumption
3
holds.
Define
U_{\delta,L}:=2LU_{r}+2U_{\omega}.

For any
t\geq 0,L\geq 0,k\in[K]
, it holds that

\displaystyle|\delta^{k}_{t,L}|\leq U_{\delta,L},~~~~\text{and}~~~\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\|\leq U_{\delta,L}.

(98)

Proof.

From Eq. (
6
),

\displaystyle|\delta^{k}_{t,L}|

\displaystyle=|\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-\eta_{t}^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}|

\displaystyle\leq|\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-\eta_{t}^{k})|+|(\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}|

\displaystyle\leq 2LU_{r}+2U_{\omega},

where the last inequality holds due to Lemma
H.1
and
\|\phi(s^{k}_{t+1})-\phi(s^{k}_{t})\|\leq 2
.

Recall from Eq. (
B
) that

\displaystyle{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left((r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}).

So,

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\|

\displaystyle=\|\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left((r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})\|+\|(\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2LU_{r}+2U_{\omega}=U_{\delta,L}.

∎

Appendix I
Other Supporting Results

Proof of Proposition
1
.

It follows from Eq. (
6
) that for any
t
and
L
:

\displaystyle\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})=\left(\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-\eta_{t}^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left((r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})+\left(\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(J^{k}-\eta_{t}^{k})\right)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}}_{\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}}x_{t}^{k}\quad\quad~~~\text{(recall that $x_{t}^{k}=\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}$)}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left((r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})}_{{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}}+Ly_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}+{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}+Ly_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}),

proving Eq. (
17
).
In the remaining proof, we focus on proving the second decomposition in Eq. (
18
).
We have,

\displaystyle\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})=\left(\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-\eta_{t}^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left((r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})+\left(\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(J^{k}-\eta_{t}^{k})\right)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left((r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left((R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})-J^{k})+(\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)\phi(s^{(0)})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left((R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})-J^{k})+(\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)\phi(s^{(0)})

\displaystyle+L(J^{k}-\eta_{t}^{k})\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}).

Under steady-state distribution, by the Bellman equation, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left((R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})-J^{k})+(\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}z^{k,*}\right)\phi(s^{(0)})=0.

(99)

Then,

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left((R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})-J^{k})+(\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)\phi(s^{(0)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left((R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})-J^{k})+(\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)\phi(s^{(0)})

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left((R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})-J^{k})+(\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}z^{k,*}\right)\phi(s^{(0)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}(\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}x_{t}^{k}\phi(s^{(0)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[(\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}x_{t}^{k}\phi(s^{(0)})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})(\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}\right]}_{A_{L}^{k}}x_{t}^{k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}.

Then,

\displaystyle\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})=\xi_{t,L}^{k}+Ly_{t}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})+A_{L}^{k}x_{t}^{k},

where

\displaystyle\xi_{t,L}^{k}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left((r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left((r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})-J^{k})+(\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)\phi(s^{(0)}).

∎

Lemma I.1
.

Fix
\tau\in{\mathbb{N}}
.
When
\frac{\zeta U_{\delta,L}U_{\omega}}{L}\leq 1/2
,
for any
t\geq\tau>0
and
k\in[K]
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\leq\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}\beta,~~\text{and }~\|\mathbf{B}_{t}-\mathbf{B}_{t-\tau}\|\leq 2\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\zeta+4\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}.

Proof.

For any
t\geq\tau>0
and
k\in[K]
, we have

\displaystyle\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\leq\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|\omega_{i+1}^{k}-\omega_{i}^{k}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|\Pi_{U_{\omega}}\left(\omega^{k}_{i}+\beta\frac{1}{L}\delta_{i,L}^{k}\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\top}\phi(s_{i,0}^{k})\right)-\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\omega_{i}^{k})\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|\beta\frac{1}{L}\delta_{i,L}^{k}\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\top}\phi(s_{i,0}^{k})\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}\beta,

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
H.2
.
In addition,

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|\leq\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{i+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|(\mathbf{B}_{i}+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{i,L}^{k}\phi(s_{i,0}^{k})(\omega_{i}^{k})^{\top}){\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|(\mathbf{B}_{i}+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{i,L}^{k}\phi(s_{i,0}^{k})(\omega_{i}^{k})^{\top}){\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{i}{\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}+{\mathbf{B}}_{i}{\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{i,L}^{k}\phi(s_{i,0}^{k})(\omega_{i}^{k})^{\top}\|\|{\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}\|+\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|{\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{I}}\|\|{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\zeta\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|{\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}\|+\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|{\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{I}}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\zeta\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\frac{1}{1-2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{i}\|_{F}^{2}}+\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}4\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{i}\|_{F}^{2},

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
C.1
, and
{\mathbf{Q}}_{i}
is defined in Eq. (
19
) as

\displaystyle{\mathbf{Q}}_{i}=\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{i,L}^{k}\phi(s_{i,0}^{k})(\omega_{i}^{k})^{\top}.

We have

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{i}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{i,\perp}\delta_{i,L}^{k}\phi(s_{i}^{k})(\omega_{i}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\delta_{i,L}^{k}|\|{\mathbf{P}}_{i,\perp}\|\|\phi(s_{i}^{k})\|\|\omega_{i}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq\zeta\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}.

(100)

When
\zeta\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\leq 1/2
,
1-2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{i}\|_{F}^{2}\geq 1/2
.
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|\leq

\displaystyle 2\tau\zeta\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}+4\tau\zeta^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}.

∎

Lemma I.2
.

Fix
\tau\in{\mathbb{N}}
.
For any
k\in[K]
and any
j\geq t-\tau>\tau
, where
t\geq 2\tau
, it holds that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}(\tau)\frac{\beta^{2}}{L}+C_{2}(\tau)\frac{\zeta\beta}{L}+C_{3}(\tau)\gamma\beta+C_{4}\frac{\beta}{\sqrt{L}}+2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{5}(\tau^{2})\frac{\beta^{4}}{L^{2}}+C_{6}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}+8\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+C_{7}\frac{\beta^{2}}{L}+C_{8}(\tau^{2})\gamma^{2}\beta^{2}+2\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2},

where

\displaystyle C_{1}(\tau)=2\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L},C_{2}(\tau)=8\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}^{2},C_{3}(\tau)=2\tau U_{r},C_{4}=\left(U_{\delta}+U_{\delta}\sqrt{\frac{2m\rho}{1-\rho}}\right),

\displaystyle C_{5}(\tau^{2})=48\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}},C_{6}(\tau^{2})=768\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{3}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}},C_{7}=3U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho}),C_{8}(\tau^{2})=24\tau^{2}U_{r}^{2}.

(101)

Proof.

For any
j
, one has

\displaystyle\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|=\|\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\omega_{j}^{k}+\frac{\beta}{L}\delta_{j,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k}))-\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\omega_{j}^{k})\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})\|\qquad\qquad\text{(by non-expansive property of projection)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}\left(\widetilde{A}_{j,L}^{k}x_{j}^{k}+{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}+Ly_{j}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})\right)\|~~~~~~\text{(by Proposition \ref{prop: key intermediate})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}{\widetilde{A}_{j,L}^{k}}x_{j}^{k}\|+\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}\|+\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}Ly_{j}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\frac{2\beta}{L}}\|x_{j}^{k}\|+\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}\|+\beta|y_{j}^{k}|.

(102)

By Lemma
I.5
,
and by the tower property and the notation defined in Section
B
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq\left(U_{\delta}+U_{\delta}\sqrt{\frac{2m\rho}{1-\rho}}\right)\sqrt{L},

where
U_{\delta}=2U_{r}+2U_{\omega}
as defined in Lemma
I.5
.
Then, taking expectation up to
t-2\tau
, Eq. (
I
) can be written as,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|\leq{\frac{2\beta}{L}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{j}^{k}\|+\frac{\beta}{\sqrt{L}}\left(U_{\delta}+U_{\delta}\sqrt{\frac{2m\rho}{1-\rho}}\right)+\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{j}^{k}|.

(103)

By Lemma
I.4
, Eq. (
103
) can be written as,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|\leq 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{j}^{k}\|+\frac{\beta}{\sqrt{L}}\left(U_{\delta}+U_{\delta}\sqrt{\frac{2m\rho}{1-\rho}}\right)+\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{j}^{k}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\left(\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}\beta+4\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|\right)+\frac{\beta}{\sqrt{L}}\left(U_{\delta}+U_{\delta}\sqrt{\frac{2m\rho}{1-\rho}}\right)

\displaystyle+\beta\left(2\tau U_{r}\gamma+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{2\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}}_{:=C_{1}(\tau)}{\frac{\beta^{2}}{L}}+\underbrace{8\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}^{2}}_{:=C_{2}(\tau)}\frac{\zeta\beta}{L}+\underbrace{2\tau U_{r}}_{:=C_{3}(\tau)}\gamma\beta+\frac{\beta}{\sqrt{L}}\underbrace{\left(U_{\delta}+U_{\delta}\sqrt{\frac{2m\rho}{1-\rho}}\right)}_{:=C_{4}}+2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{1}(\tau)\frac{\beta^{2}}{L}+C_{2}(\tau)\frac{\zeta\beta}{L}+C_{3}(\tau)\gamma\beta+C_{4}\frac{\beta}{\sqrt{L}}+2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|.

Similarly,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}={\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\omega_{j}^{k}+\frac{\beta}{L}\delta_{j,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k}))-\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\omega_{j}^{k})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}\left(\widetilde{A}_{j,L}^{k}x_{j}^{k}+{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}+Ly_{j}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})\right)\|^{2}\quad\quad~~~~~~\text{(by Proposition \ref{prop: key intermediate})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}{\widetilde{A}_{j,L}^{k}}x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+3\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}\|^{2}+3\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}Ly_{j}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\frac{12\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+3\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}\|^{2}+3\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{j}^{k}|^{2}.

(104)

By Lemma
I.5
and the tower property, we get,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}\leq U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})L.

By Lemma
I.4
, Eq. (
I
) can be written as,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\leq 3\left({\frac{4\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})\frac{\beta^{2}}{L}+\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{j}^{k}|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 48\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{4}}\beta^{4}+768\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{3}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{4}}\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}+24\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})\frac{\beta^{2}}{L}+24\tau^{2}U_{r}^{2}\gamma^{2}\beta^{2}+6\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}.

∎

Lemma I.3
.

Fix
\tau>0
.
Choosing
\zeta
so that
\zeta\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\leq 1/2
and
U_{\omega}\zeta\leq 1/2
,
for any
j\geq t-\tau\geq\tau
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|\mathbf{B}_{j+1}-\mathbf{B}_{j}\|]\leq

\displaystyle{C_{9}(\tau)}\frac{\beta\zeta}{L}+{C_{10}(\tau)}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+4U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+{C_{11}(\tau)}\zeta\beta+{C_{12}}\frac{\zeta}{\sqrt{K}}

\displaystyle+{C_{13}}\zeta m\rho^{\tau}+{C_{14}(\tau)}\gamma\zeta+2U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|+{C_{15}}\zeta^{2},

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|\mathbf{B}_{j+1}-\mathbf{B}_{j}\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C_{16}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}+C_{17}(\tau^{2})\frac{\zeta^{4}}{L^{2}}+C_{18}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+C_{19}(\tau^{2})\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}+C_{20}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}

\displaystyle+C_{21}\zeta^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}+C_{22}(\tau^{2})\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2}+C_{23}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}+C_{24}\zeta^{4},

where

\displaystyle C_{9}(\tau)=4U_{\omega}\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L},C_{10}(\tau)=16\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}^{3},C_{11}(\tau)=2\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\tau,C_{12}=2U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L},

\displaystyle C_{13}=4\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega},C_{14}(\tau)=4U_{\omega}\tau U_{r},C_{15}=4\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L}U_{\omega}^{2},

\displaystyle C_{16}(\tau^{2})=384\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}},C_{17}(\tau^{2})=6144\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{5}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}},C_{18}=192U_{\omega}^{2},C_{19}=\frac{48U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}\tau^{2},C_{20}=\frac{48U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}},

\displaystyle C_{21}=\frac{48U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}},C_{22}(\tau^{2})=192U_{\omega}^{2}\tau^{2}U_{r}^{2},C_{23}=48U_{\omega}^{2},C_{24}=32\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}U_{\omega}^{4}.

Proof.

Fix
\tau>0
. For any
j\geq t-\tau
, the one-step perturbation of
{\mathbf{B}}_{j}
can be bounded as

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{B}_{j}+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}){\mathbf{R}}_{j+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{B}_{j}+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}){\mathbf{R}}_{j+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}{\mathbf{R}}_{j+1}^{-1}+{\mathbf{B}}_{j}{\mathbf{R}}_{j+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|\left\|{\mathbf{R}}_{j+1}^{-1}\right\|+\|{\mathbf{R}}_{j+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{I}}\|\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|\frac{1}{1-2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{j}\|_{F}^{2}}+4\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{j}\|_{F}^{2},

(105)

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
C.1
and the fact that
\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|\leq 1
.
From Eq. (
19
), we know

\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{j}\|_{F}\leq\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\zeta.
When
\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\zeta\leq 1/2
, it holds that
\frac{1}{1-2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{j}\|_{F}^{2}}\leq 2
.
Thus, it remains to bound
\left\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|
, for which we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left({\widetilde{A}_{j,L}^{k}}x_{j}^{k}+{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}+Ly_{j}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})\right)(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\widetilde{A}_{j,L}^{k}}x_{j}^{k}(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}Ly_{j}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|x_{j}^{k}\|+\underbrace{\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}(\omega_{j}^{k}-\omega_{j-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\right\|}_{I_{1}}+\underbrace{\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}(\omega_{j-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\right\|}_{I_{2}}+U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}|y_{j}^{k}|.

(106)

For
I_{1}
in Eq. (
I
),

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}(\omega_{j}^{k}-\omega_{j-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\right\|\leq\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\tau\zeta\beta.~~~~~(\text{ by Lemma \ref{lmm: crude parameter bound}})

(107)

For
I_{2}
in Eq. (
I
), taking conditional expectation with respect to the Markovian trajectories up to time
j-\tau
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}{\mathbb{E}}_{j-\tau}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}(\omega_{j-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{j-\tau}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}(\omega_{j-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}~~~~(\text{by Jensen's inequality for expectation})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{j-\tau}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left\|{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}(\omega_{j-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}+2\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{k^{\prime}=k+1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{j-\tau}\left\langle{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}(\omega_{j-\tau}^{k})^{\top},{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k^{\prime}}(\omega_{j-\tau}^{k^{\prime}})^{\top}\right\rangle\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left(KU_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}+2\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{k^{\prime}=k+1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{j-\tau}\left[{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}(\omega_{j-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\right]{\mathbb{E}}_{j-\tau}\left[{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k^{\prime}}(\omega_{j-\tau}^{k^{\prime}})^{\top}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left(KU_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}+K^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}\frac{\zeta}{\sqrt{K}}+\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\zeta m\rho^{\tau}.

(108)

where inequality (a) follows from Lemma
H.2
and conditional independence.

Plugging Eq. (
107
) and Eq. (
I
) back to Eq. (
I
) and Eq. (
I
), we get, under expectation,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{j-\tau}[\|\mathbf{B}_{j+1}-\mathbf{B}_{j}\|]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}[\|\mathbf{B}_{j+1}-\mathbf{B}_{j}\|\mid v_{0:j-\tau}]

\displaystyle\leq 4U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{j-\tau}\|x_{j}^{k}\|+2\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\tau\zeta\beta+2U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}\frac{\zeta}{\sqrt{K}}

\displaystyle\quad+4\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\zeta m\rho^{\tau}+2U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{j-\tau}|y_{j}^{k}|+4\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}.

(109)

Finally,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|\mathbf{B}_{j+1}-\mathbf{B}_{j}\|]={\mathbb{E}}[\|\mathbf{B}_{j+1}-\mathbf{B}_{j}\|\mid v_{0:t-2\tau}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[~{\mathbb{E}}[\|\mathbf{B}_{j+1}-\mathbf{B}_{j}\|\mid v_{0:j-\tau}]\mid v_{0:t-2\tau}]\quad\quad~~~~~\text{(by the tower property of conditional expecation)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[{\mathbb{E}}_{j-\tau}\|x_{j}^{k}\|]+2\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\tau\zeta\beta+2U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}\frac{\zeta}{\sqrt{K}}\quad~~~~~\text{(by applying Eq.\,(\ref{eqn: one step B before substituting jth}))}

\displaystyle+4\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\zeta m\rho^{\tau}+2U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[{\mathbb{E}}_{j-\tau}|y_{j}^{k}|]+4\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[\|x_{j}^{k}\|]+2\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\tau\zeta\beta+2U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}\frac{\zeta}{\sqrt{K}}

\displaystyle+4\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\zeta m\rho^{\tau}+2U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}[|y_{j}^{k}|]+4\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{L}\left(\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}\beta+4\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta+\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|\right)+2\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\tau\zeta\beta+2U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}\frac{\zeta}{\sqrt{K}}

\displaystyle+4\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\zeta m\rho^{\tau}+2U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(2\tau U_{r}\gamma+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|\right)+4\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}\quad\quad~~~~~\text{(applying Lemma \ref{lmm: jth to tth error bound})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{4U_{\omega}\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}}_{:=C_{9}(\tau)}\frac{\beta\zeta}{L}+\underbrace{16\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}^{3}}_{:=C_{10}(\tau)}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+4U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+\underbrace{2\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\tau}_{:=C_{11}(\tau)}\zeta\beta+\underbrace{2U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}}_{:=C_{12}}\frac{\zeta}{\sqrt{K}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{4\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}}_{:=C_{13}}\zeta m\rho^{\tau}+\underbrace{4U_{\omega}\tau U_{r}}_{:=C_{14}(\tau)}\gamma\zeta+2U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|+\underbrace{4\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L}U_{\omega}^{2}}_{:=C_{15}}\zeta^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{C_{9}(\tau)}\frac{\beta\zeta}{L}+{C_{10}(\tau)}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+4U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+{C_{11}(\tau)}\zeta\beta+{C_{12}}\frac{\zeta}{\sqrt{K}}

\displaystyle+{C_{13}}\zeta m\rho^{\tau}+{C_{14}(\tau)}\gamma\zeta+2U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|+{C_{15}}\zeta^{2}.

Analogously,
