\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}\left(\frac{1}{1-2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{j}\|_{F}^{2}}\right)^{2}+32\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{j}\|_{F}^{4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8\left\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}+32\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{j}\|_{F}^{4}.

(110)

Similar to Eq. (
I
), the first term can be decomposed and bounded as,

\displaystyle 8\left\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{24\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\widetilde{A}_{j,L}^{k}}x_{j}^{k}(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}+\frac{24\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}+\frac{24\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}Ly_{j}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 96U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+\underbrace{\frac{48\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}(\omega_{j}^{k}-\omega_{j-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}}_{I_{3}}+\underbrace{\frac{48\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}(\omega_{j-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}}_{I_{4}}+U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{24\zeta^{2}}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}|y_{j}^{k}|^{2}.

(111)

For
I_{3}
in Eq. (
I
), by Lemma
H.2
and Lemma
I.1
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{48\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}(\omega_{j}^{k}-\omega_{j-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{48U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}\tau^{2}\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}.

(112)

For
I_{4}
in Eq. (
I
), we take the conditional expectation with respect to the Markovian trajectories up to time
j-\tau
. Similar to Eq. (
I
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{48\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{j-\tau}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}(\omega_{j-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{48\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}\left(KU_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}+K^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{48U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}+\frac{48U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\zeta^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}.

(113)

Plugging Eq. (
112
) and Eq. (
I
) back to Eq. (
I
) and Eq. (
I
), and applying the tower property, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}={\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\left[{\mathbb{E}}_{j-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 96U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{48U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}\tau^{2}\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}+\frac{48U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}

\displaystyle+\frac{48U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\zeta^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}+U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{24\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{j}^{k}|^{2}+32(\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\zeta)^{4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 96U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\left(4\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\beta^{2}+64\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{3}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\zeta^{2}+2\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}\right)+\frac{48U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}\tau^{2}\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}+\frac{48U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}

\displaystyle+\frac{48U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\zeta^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}+U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{24\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(8\tau^{2}U_{r}^{2}\gamma^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}\right)+32(\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\zeta)^{4}~~~~~\text{(applying Lemma \ref{lmm: jth to tth error bound})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 384\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}+6144\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{5}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{\zeta 4}{L^{2}}+192U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{48U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}\tau^{2}\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}+\frac{48U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}

\displaystyle+\frac{48U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\zeta^{2}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau}+192U_{\omega}^{2}\tau^{2}U_{r}^{2}\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2}+48U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}+32\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{4}}{L^{4}}U_{\omega}^{4}\zeta^{4}.

∎

Lemma I.4
.

For
0\leq t-\tau\leq j\leq t-1
, we have for any
k\in[K]
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{j}^{k}\|\leq\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}\beta+4\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\leq 4\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\beta^{2}+64\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{3}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\zeta^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2},

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{j}^{k}|\leq 2\tau U_{r}\gamma+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{j}^{k}|^{2}\leq 8\tau^{2}U_{r}^{2}\gamma^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2},

when
4\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{3}\zeta\leq 2\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}^{2}
, and
128\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{5}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\zeta^{2}\leq 32\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{3}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}
.

Lemma
I.4
aligns the error terms indexed by
j
to a common reference
time
t
, which is required for a consistent Lyapunov analysis.
The bounds follow from the triangle inequality applied along the trajectory from
j
to
t
;
since
j\geq t-\tau
, the difference between
x_{j}^{k}
(resp.,
y_{j}^{k}
) and
x_{t}^{k}
(resp.,
y_{t}^{k}
)
accumulates at most
\tau
one-step perturbations.
Each perturbation is upper bounded using the update dynamics, yielding deviations that scale
linearly in
\tau
, while
{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|
and
{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|
serve as anchored
reference terms.

Proof.

For any
t-1\geq j\geq t-\tau
, we have

\displaystyle\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\leq 2\|x_{j}^{k}-x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+2\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+2\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+4\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{j}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+2\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+4U_{\omega}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}+2\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\beta^{2}+4\tau^{2}U_{\omega}(2\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}\zeta+4\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2})^{2}+2\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}~~~~~(\text{by Lemma \ref{lmm: crude parameter bound}}).

Then, taking expectation, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\leq 4\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\beta^{2}+32\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{3}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\zeta^{2}+128\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{5}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\zeta^{4}+2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\beta^{2}+64\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{3}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\zeta^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}.

The last inequality holds since
128\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{5}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}\zeta^{2}\leq 32\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{3}\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}
.

Analogously, we get,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{j}^{k}\|\leq\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}\beta+2\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta+4\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{3}\zeta^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}\beta+4\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

The last inequality holds since
4\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{3}\zeta\leq 2\tau\frac{U_{\delta,L}}{L}U_{\omega}^{2}
.

Similarly, for
|y_{j}^{k}|
, we have,

\displaystyle|y_{j}^{k}|^{2}\leq 2|y_{j}^{k}-y_{t}^{k}|^{2}+2|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\tau\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}|y_{i+1}^{k}-y_{i}^{k}|^{2}+2|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\tau\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}|\eta_{i+1}^{k}-\eta_{i}^{k}|^{2}+|y_{t}^{k}|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\tau\gamma^{2}\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}\left|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}r_{t,\ell}^{k}-\eta^{k}_{t}\right|^{2}+2|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8\tau^{2}U_{r}^{2}\gamma^{2}+2|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}.

Taking expectation, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{j}^{k}|^{2}\leq 8\tau^{2}U_{r}^{2}\gamma^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|^{2}.

Analogously, we get,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{j}^{k}|\leq 2\tau U_{r}\gamma+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-2\tau}|y_{t}^{k}|.

∎

Lemma I.5
.

For
k\in[K]
,
t\geq 0
,
L>0
, and any state
s_{t,0}^{k}
,
we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\|\right]\leq\left(U_{\delta}+U_{\delta}\sqrt{\frac{2m\rho}{1-\rho}}\right)\sqrt{L},

(114)

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}\leq U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})L,

(115)

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|\leq\sqrt{16U_{r}^{2}+32U_{r}^{2}\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho}}\sqrt{L},

(116)

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|^{2}\leq\left(16U_{r}^{2}+32U_{r}^{2}\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho}\right)L,

(117)

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|{\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}}\right\|\leq\sqrt{16+32\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho}}\sqrt{L},

(118)

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|{\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}}\right\|^{2}\leq\left(16+32\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho}\right)L,

(119)

where
U_{\delta}:=2U_{r}+2U_{\omega}
, and the expectation is taken over the Markovian sampling trajectories.

Proof.

We know that
V(s)=\phi(s)^{\top}z^{k,*}={\mathbb{E}}_{\pi\otimes P^{k}}[R(s,a)-J^{k}+V(s^{\prime})]
, which implies for any
s
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{s\sim u^{k},(a,s^{\prime})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}}[R(s,a)-J^{k}+(\phi(s^{\prime})-\phi(s))^{\top}z^{k,*}]=0.

(120)

Then, for
\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\|
, taking expectation, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\|\leq\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}}~~~~(\text{by Jensen's inequality})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left[(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right]\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Bigg(\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\left[(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right]\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\ell+1}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}\bigg\langle\left[(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right]\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}),

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left[(r_{t,\ell^{\prime}}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell^{\prime}+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell^{\prime}}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right]\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\bigg\rangle\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg(\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\left[(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right]\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\ell+1}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bigg\langle\left[(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right]\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}),\right.\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.\left.\left[(r_{t,\ell^{\prime}}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell^{\prime}+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell^{\prime}}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right]\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\bigg\rangle\mid v_{0:{(j,\ell)}}\right]\right]\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Bigg(U_{\delta}^{2}L+2\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\ell+1}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\langle\left[(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right]\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}),\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.{\mathbb{E}}[\left[(r_{t,\ell^{\prime}}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell^{\prime}+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell^{\prime}}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right]\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\mid v_{0:{(j,\ell)}}]\right]\rangle\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Bigg(U_{\delta}^{2}L+2\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\ell+1}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}[\left\|{\left[(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right]\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})}\right\|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\|{\mathbb{E}}[\left[(r_{t,\ell^{\prime}}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell^{\prime}+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell^{\prime}}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right]\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\mid v_{0:{(t,\ell)}}]\|]\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}(\text{by Cauchy-Schwarz})

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sqrt{U_{\delta}^{2}L+2\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\ell+1}^{L-1}U_{\delta}\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left[(r_{t,\ell^{\prime}}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell^{\prime}+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell^{\prime}}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right]\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\mid v_{0:{(t,\ell)}}\right]\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{U_{\delta}^{2}L+2\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\ell+1}^{L-1}U_{\delta}^{2}m\rho^{\ell^{\prime}-\ell}}~~~~~~~~~~(\text{by the ergodicity Assumption})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle U_{\delta}\sqrt{L}+\sqrt{2U_{\delta}^{2}m\frac{\rho L}{1-\rho}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(U_{\delta}+U_{\delta}\sqrt{\frac{2m\rho}{1-\rho}}\right)\sqrt{L},

where inequality (a) holds because
\left\|{\left[(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\right]\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})}\right\|\leq U_{\delta}
.

Similarly, for Eq. (
115
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}\leq U_{\delta}^{2}L+2U_{\delta}^{2}\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho}L=U_{\delta}^{2}(1+2\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho})L.

Now we prove Eq. (
117
).
For ease of exposition, we omit the explicit specification of the underlying distributions in the expectation when it is clear from the context.

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|{\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})-J^{k})\phi(s^{(0)})\right]}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|{\left[(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[(r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})-J^{k})\phi(s^{(0)})\right]\right]}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\ell+1}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}\big\langle(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[(r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})-J^{k})\phi(s^{(0)})\right],

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad(r_{t,\ell^{\prime}}^{k}-J^{k})\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[(r(s^{(\ell^{\prime})},a^{(\ell^{\prime})})-J^{k})\phi(s^{(0)})\right]\big\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 16U_{r}^{2}L+32U_{r}^{2}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\ell+1}^{L-1}m\rho^{\ell^{\prime}-\ell}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(16U_{r}^{2}+32U_{r}^{2}\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho}\right)L.

(a) follows analogously to the proof of Eq. (
114
).

For Eq. (
116
), by Jensen’s inequality, we easily get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|\leq\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\|^{2}}\leq\sqrt{16U_{r}^{2}+32U_{r}^{2}\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho}}\sqrt{L}.

Similarly, for Eq. (
119
), we have,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|{\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|{\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}-\mathbb{E}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}[\phi(s^{(0)})(\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|{\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}-\mathbb{E}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}[\phi(s^{(0)})(\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}-\mathbb{E}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}[\phi(s^{(0)})(\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top},\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}-\mathbb{E}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}[\phi(s^{(0)})(\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 16L+32\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\ell+1}^{L-1}m\rho^{\ell^{\prime}-\ell}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(16+32\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho}\right)L.

By Jensen’s inequality, Eq. (
118
) can be proved,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|{\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}}\right\|\leq\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\left\|{\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L}^{k}}\right\|^{2}}\leq\sqrt{16+32\frac{m\rho}{1-\rho}}\sqrt{L}.

∎

Lemma I.6
.

Suppose that Assumption
2
.
Fix
\tau>0
. For any
t\geq\tau
,
L>0
, and
k\in[K]
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big]\|\leq 4LU_{r}m\rho^{\tau}

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{A}_{L}^{k}\big]\|\leq 4m\rho^{\tau}

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}]\|\leq 2U_{\delta,L}m\rho^{\tau}.

Proof.

Recall from Eq. (
B
) that

\displaystyle{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\big((r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\big)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}),

and the average-reward Bellman equation

\mathbb{E}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(r(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})-J^{k})+(\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}B^{*}w^{k,*}\phi(s^{(0)})\right]=0.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}]\|=

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\big((r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})+(\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega^{k,*}\big)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right]\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.-\mathbb{E}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(r(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})-J^{k})+(\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}B^{*}w^{k,*}\phi(s^{(0)})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2U_{\delta,L}\cdot d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{0:\tau}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k},a_{t,1}^{k},s_{t,1}^{k},\cdots,a_{t,L-1}^{k},s_{t,L}^{k}\mid s_{t-\tau}^{k}=s),\mu^{k}\otimes(\pi\otimes P^{k})^{L-1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2U_{\delta,L}m\rho^{\tau},

where the notation
\mathbb{P}_{0:\tau}^{k}
is defined in Assumption
2
, the first inequality follows from Lemma
H.2
, and the last inequality follows from Assumption
2
and the fact that
L
-block of a Markov chain mixes at the same rate as the original chain.

Recall from Eq. (
14
) that

\displaystyle\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(r_{t,\ell}^{k}-J^{k})\phi(s_{t,0}^{k});\quad\bar{b}_{L}^{k}={\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})-J^{k})\phi(s^{(0)})\right].

Note that
\left|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}\right|\leq 2LU_{r}.
Similar to the above derivation, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L}^{k}\big]\|

\displaystyle\leq 2LU_{r}2\cdot d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{0:\tau}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k},a_{t,1}^{k},s_{t,1}^{k},\cdots,a_{t,L-1}^{k},s_{t,L}^{k}\mid s_{t-\tau}^{k}=s),\mu^{k}\otimes(\pi\otimes P^{k})^{L-1})

\displaystyle\leq 4LU_{r}m\rho^{\tau}.

Recall from Eq. (
B
) that
\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}=\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}
. Since
\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}\|\leq 2
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{A}_{L}^{k}\big]\|\leq 4m\rho^{\tau}.

∎

I.1
Proof of Eq. (
31
)

Proof.

Recall that
{\mathcal{R}}
is the optimal rotation matrix that aligns
{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}
and
{\mathbf{B}}^{*}
in the sense that
{\mathcal{R}}=\text{argmin}_{{\mathcal{R}}^{\top}{\mathcal{R}}={\mathbf{I}}}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\|_{F}
. That is,
{\mathcal{R}}={\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}
, where
{\mathbf{U}}\Sigma{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}={\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}
is the SVD. We have

\displaystyle{\mathbf{I}}-({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbf{I}}-{\mathcal{R}}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbf{I}}-({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}\Sigma{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbf{I}}-{\mathbf{V}}\Sigma{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}.

For any given matrix
{\mathbf{A}}
, let
\sigma_{i}({\mathbf{A}})
denote its
i
-th largest singular value. Since multiplying an orthogonal matrix does not change the singular values, the singular values of
{\mathbf{I}}-({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}
can be bounded as follows,

\displaystyle\sigma_{i}({\mathbf{I}}-{\mathbf{V}}\Sigma{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})=\sigma_{i}({\mathbf{V}}({\mathbf{I}}-\Sigma){\mathbf{V}}^{\top})=\sigma_{i}({\mathbf{I}}-\Sigma)=1-\cos(\theta_{i}),

where
\cos(\theta_{i})
are the singular values of the matrix
({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}
, and
\theta_{i}
are the principal angles between the two subspaces of interest. Then

\displaystyle\sigma_{i}({\mathbf{I}}-({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*})=1-\cos(\theta_{i})\leq 1-\cos^{2}(\theta_{i})=\sin^{2}(\theta_{i}).

Recall
\sigma_{i}({\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1})=\sin(\theta_{i})
. All the singular values of
{\mathbf{I}}-({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}
are smaller than the largest singular value of
{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}
squared, i.e.,

\displaystyle\sigma_{i}({\mathbf{I}}-({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*})\leq\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\|^{2},

which implies

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{I}}-({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\|\leq\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\|^{2}.

∎
